"""Verbatim function extracts from the official PaCMAP 0.9.1 source.

The functions below are copied without changing their signatures or bodies.
Only this file-level provenance note was added. See SOURCE.txt for the exact
upstream URL and function map.
"""

import logging
import math
import time

import numba
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import TruncatedSVD, PCA
from sklearn import preprocessing

# Mandatory FAISS import (default backend)
import faiss
# Optional imports for annoy and voyager
try:
    from annoy import AnnoyIndex
except ImportError:
    AnnoyIndex = None

try:
    import voyager
except ImportError:
    voyager = None

global _RANDOM_STATE
_RANDOM_STATE = None

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@numba.njit("f4(f4[:])", cache=True)
def l2_norm(x):
    """
    L2 norm of a vector.
    """
    result = 0.0
    for i in range(x.shape[0]):
        result += x[i] ** 2
    return np.sqrt(result)


@numba.njit("f4(f4[:],f4[:])", cache=True)
def euclid_dist(x1, x2):
    """
    Euclidean distance between two vectors.
    """
    result = 0.0
    for i in range(x1.shape[0]):
        result += (x1[i] - x2[i]) ** 2
    return np.sqrt(result)


@numba.njit("f4(f4[:],f4[:])", cache=True)
def manhattan_dist(x1, x2):
    """
    Manhattan distance between two vectors.
    """
    result = 0.0
    for i in range(x1.shape[0]):
        result += np.abs(x1[i] - x2[i])
    return result


@numba.njit("f4(f4[:],f4[:])", cache=True)
def angular_dist(x1, x2):
    """
    Angular (i.e. cosine) distance between two vectors.
    """
    x1_norm = np.maximum(l2_norm(x1), 1e-20)
    x2_norm = np.maximum(l2_norm(x2), 1e-20)
    result = 0.0
    for i in range(x1.shape[0]):
        result += x1[i] * x2[i]
    return np.sqrt(2.0 - 2.0 * result / x1_norm / x2_norm)


@numba.njit("f4(f4[:],f4[:])", cache=True)
def hamming_dist(x1, x2):
    """
    Hamming distance between two vectors.
    """
    result = 0.0
    for i in range(x1.shape[0]):
        if x1[i] != x2[i]:
            result += 1.0
    return result


@numba.njit(cache=True)
def calculate_dist(x1, x2, distance_index):
    if distance_index == 0:  # euclidean
        return euclid_dist(x1, x2)
    elif distance_index == 1:  # manhattan
        return manhattan_dist(x1, x2)
    elif distance_index == 2:  # angular
        return angular_dist(x1, x2)
    elif distance_index == 3:  # hamming
        return hamming_dist(x1, x2)


@numba.njit("i4[:](i4,i4,i4[:],i4)", nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_FP(n_samples, maximum, reject_ind, self_ind):
    """Sample `n_samples` samples from `maximum` points, excluding the `reject_ind` points."""
    result = np.empty(n_samples, dtype=np.int32)
    for i in range(n_samples):
        reject_sample = True
        while reject_sample:
            j = np.random.randint(maximum)
            if j == self_ind:
                continue
            for k in range(i):
                if j == result[k]:
                    break
            else:
                for k in range(reject_ind.shape[0]):
                    if j == reject_ind[k]:
                        break
                else:
                    reject_sample = False
        result[i] = j
    return result


@numba.njit("i4[:,:](f4[:,:],f4[:,:],i4[:,:],i4)", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_neighbors_pair(X, scaled_dist, nbrs, n_neighbors):
    n = X.shape[0]
    pair_neighbors = np.empty((n * n_neighbors, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        scaled_sort = np.argsort(scaled_dist[i])
        for j in numba.prange(n_neighbors):
            pair_neighbors[i*n_neighbors + j][0] = i
            pair_neighbors[i*n_neighbors + j][1] = nbrs[i][scaled_sort[j]]
    return pair_neighbors


@numba.njit("i4[:,:](f4[:,:],i4,i4)", nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_MN_pair(X, n_MN, option=0):
    '''Sample Mid Near pairs.'''
    n = X.shape[0]
    pair_MN = np.empty((n * n_MN, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        for j in range(n_MN):
            sampled = sample_FP(
                n_samples=6,
                maximum=n,
                reject_ind=pair_MN[i * n_MN:i * n_MN + j, 1],
                self_ind=i
            )
            dist_list = np.empty((6), dtype=np.float32)
            for t in range(sampled.shape[0]):
                dist_list[t] = calculate_dist(
                    X[i], X[sampled[t]], distance_index=option)
            min_dic = np.argmin(dist_list)
            dist_list = np.delete(dist_list, [min_dic])
            sampled = np.delete(sampled, [min_dic])
            picked = sampled[np.argmin(dist_list)]
            pair_MN[i * n_MN + j][0] = i
            pair_MN[i * n_MN + j][1] = picked
    return pair_MN


@numba.njit("i4[:,:](f4[:,:],i4,i4,i4)", nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_MN_pair_deterministic(X, n_MN, random_state, option=0):
    '''Sample Mid Near pairs using the given random state.'''
    n = X.shape[0]
    pair_MN = np.empty((n * n_MN, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        for j in range(n_MN):
            # Shifting the seed to prevent sampling the same pairs
            np.random.seed(random_state + i * n_MN + j)
            sampled = sample_FP(
                n_samples=6,
                maximum=n,
                reject_ind=pair_MN[i * n_MN:i * n_MN + j, 1],
                self_ind=i
            )
            dist_list = np.empty((6), dtype=np.float32)
            for t in range(sampled.shape[0]):
                dist_list[t] = calculate_dist(
                    X[i], X[sampled[t]], distance_index=option)
            min_dic = np.argmin(dist_list)
            dist_list = np.delete(dist_list, [min_dic])
            sampled = np.delete(sampled, [min_dic])
            picked = sampled[np.argmin(dist_list)]
            pair_MN[i * n_MN + j][0] = i
            pair_MN[i * n_MN + j][1] = picked
    return pair_MN


@numba.njit("i4[:,:](f4[:,:],i4[:,:],i4,i4)", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_FP_pair(X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP):
    '''Sample Further pairs.'''
    n = X.shape[0]
    pair_FP = np.empty((n * n_FP, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        FP_index = sample_FP(
            n_samples=n_FP,
            maximum=n,
            reject_ind=pair_neighbors[i * n_neighbors:(i + 1) * n_neighbors, 1],
            self_ind=i
        )
        for k in numba.prange(n_FP):
            pair_FP[i*n_FP + k][0] = i
            pair_FP[i*n_FP + k][1] = FP_index[k]
    return pair_FP


@numba.njit("i4[:,:](f4[:,:],i4[:,:],i4,i4,i4)", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def sample_FP_pair_deterministic(X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP, random_state):
    '''Sample Further pairs using the given random state.'''
    n = X.shape[0]
    pair_FP = np.empty((n * n_FP, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        np.random.seed(random_state + i)
        FP_index = sample_FP(
            n_samples=n_FP,
            maximum=n,
            reject_ind=pair_neighbors[i * n_neighbors:(i + 1) * n_neighbors, 1],
            self_ind=i
        )
        for k in numba.prange(n_FP):
            pair_FP[i*n_FP + k][0] = i
            pair_FP[i*n_FP + k][1] = FP_index[k]
    return pair_FP


@numba.njit("f4[:,:](f4[:,:],f4[:],i4[:,:])", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def scale_dist(knn_distance, sig, nbrs):
    '''Scale the distance'''
    n, num_neighbors = knn_distance.shape
    scaled_dist = np.zeros((n, num_neighbors), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        for j in numba.prange(num_neighbors):
            scaled_dist[i, j] = knn_distance[i, j] ** 2 / \
                sig[i] / sig[nbrs[i, j]]
    return scaled_dist


@numba.njit("void(f4[:,:],f4[:,:],f4[:,:],f4[:,:],f4,f4,f4,i4)", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def update_embedding_adam(Y, grad, m, v, beta1, beta2, lr, itr):
    '''Update the embedding with the gradient'''
    n, dim = Y.shape
    lr_t = lr * math.sqrt(1.0 - beta2 ** (itr + 1)) / (1.0 - beta1 ** (itr + 1))
    for i in numba.prange(n):
        for d in numba.prange(dim):
            m[i][d] += (1 - beta1) * (grad[i][d] - m[i][d])
            v[i][d] += (1 - beta2) * (grad[i][d]**2 - v[i][d])
            Y[i][d] -= lr_t * m[i][d]/(math.sqrt(v[i][d]) + 1e-7)


@numba.njit("f4[:,:](f4[:,:],i4[:,:],i4[:,:],i4[:,:],f4,f4,f4)", parallel=True, nogil=True, cache=True)
def pacmap_grad(Y, pair_neighbors, pair_MN, pair_FP, w_neighbors, w_MN, w_FP):
    '''Calculate the gradient for pacmap embedding given the particular set of weights.'''
    n, dim = Y.shape
    grad = np.zeros((n + 1, dim), dtype=np.float32)
    y_ij = np.empty(dim, dtype=np.float32)
    loss = np.zeros(4, dtype=np.float32)
    # NN
    for t in range(pair_neighbors.shape[0]):
        i = pair_neighbors[t, 0]
        j = pair_neighbors[t, 1]
        d_ij = 1.0
        for d in range(dim):
            y_ij[d] = Y[i, d] - Y[j, d]
            d_ij += y_ij[d] ** 2
        loss[0] += w_neighbors * (d_ij / (10. + d_ij))
        w1 = w_neighbors * (20. / (10. + d_ij) ** 2)
        for d in range(dim):
            grad[i, d] += w1 * y_ij[d]
            grad[j, d] -= w1 * y_ij[d]
    # MN
    for tt in range(pair_MN.shape[0]):
        i = pair_MN[tt, 0]
        j = pair_MN[tt, 1]
        d_ij = 1.0
        for d in range(dim):
            y_ij[d] = Y[i][d] - Y[j][d]
            d_ij += y_ij[d] ** 2
        loss[1] += w_MN * d_ij / (10000. + d_ij)
        w = w_MN * 20000. / (10000. + d_ij) ** 2
        for d in range(dim):
            grad[i, d] += w * y_ij[d]
            grad[j, d] -= w * y_ij[d]
    # FP
    for ttt in range(pair_FP.shape[0]):
        i = pair_FP[ttt, 0]
        j = pair_FP[ttt, 1]
        d_ij = 1.0
        for d in range(dim):
            y_ij[d] = Y[i, d] - Y[j, d]
            d_ij += y_ij[d] ** 2
        loss[2] += w_FP * 1. / (1. + d_ij)
        w1 = w_FP * 2. / (1. + d_ij) ** 2
        for d in range(dim):
            grad[i, d] -= w1 * y_ij[d]
            grad[j, d] += w1 * y_ij[d]
    grad[-1, 0] = loss.sum()
    return grad


def find_weight(w_MN_init, itr, *, num_iters):
    '''Find the corresponding weight given the index of an iteration'''
    (phase_1_iters, phase_2_iters, _) = num_iters
    if itr < phase_1_iters:
        w_MN = (1 - itr/phase_1_iters) * w_MN_init + itr/phase_1_iters * 3.0
        w_neighbors = 2.0
        w_FP = 1.0
    elif itr < phase_1_iters + phase_2_iters:
        w_MN = 3.0
        w_neighbors = 3
        w_FP = 1
    else:
        w_MN = 0.0
        w_neighbors = 1.
        w_FP = 1.
    return w_MN, w_neighbors, w_FP


def preprocess_X(X, distance, apply_pca, verbose, seed, high_dim, low_dim):
    '''Preprocess a dataset.
    '''
    tsvd = None
    pca_solution = False
    if distance != "hamming" and high_dim > 100 and apply_pca:
        xmin = 0  # placeholder
        xmax = 0  # placeholder
        xmean = np.mean(X, axis=0)
        X -= xmean
        tsvd = TruncatedSVD(n_components=100, random_state=seed)
        X = tsvd.fit_transform(X)
        pca_solution = True
        print_verbose("Applied PCA, the dimensionality becomes 100", verbose)
    else:
        xmin = np.min(X)
        X -= xmin
        xmax = np.max(X)
        X /= xmax
        xmean = np.mean(X, axis=0)
        X -= xmean
        tsvd = PCA(n_components=low_dim, random_state=seed)  # for init only
        tsvd.fit(X)
        print_verbose("X is normalized", verbose)
    return X, pca_solution, tsvd, xmin, xmax, xmean


def distance_to_option(distance='euclidean'):
    '''A helper function that translates distance metric to int options.
    Such a translation is useful for numba acceleration.
    '''
    if distance == 'euclidean':
        option = 0
    elif distance == 'manhattan':
        option = 1
    elif distance == 'angular':
        option = 2
    elif distance == 'hamming':
        option = 3
    else:
        raise NotImplementedError('Distance other than euclidean, manhattan,' +
                                  'angular or hamming is not supported')
    return option


def print_verbose(msg, verbose, **kwargs):
    if verbose:
        print(msg, **kwargs)


def compute_nearest_neighbors(X, n_neighbors, distance, knn_backend, random_state):
    '''Helper function to compute nearest neighbors.'''
    n, dim = X.shape
    # sample more neighbors than needed because the first one is the point itself
    k = n_neighbors + 1

    tree = None
    nbrs = None
    knn_distances = None
    
    if knn_backend == 'annoy':
        if AnnoyIndex is None:
            raise ImportError("knn_backend='annoy' requested, but annoy is not installed.")
        tree = AnnoyIndex(dim, metric=distance)
        if random_state is not None:
            tree.set_seed(random_state)
        for i in range(n):
            tree.add_item(i, X[i, :])
        tree.build(20)

        nbrs = np.zeros((n, n_neighbors), dtype=np.int32)
        knn_distances = np.empty((n, n_neighbors), dtype=np.float32)
        for i in range(n):
            nbrs_ = tree.get_nns_by_item(i, k)
            nbrs[i, :] = nbrs_[1:]
            for j in range(n_neighbors):
                knn_distances[i, j] = tree.get_distance(i, nbrs[i, j])

    elif knn_backend == 'faiss':
        if faiss is None:
            raise ImportError("faiss is not installed.")

        if random_state is not None:
            # logger.warning("Using Faiss with random state: HNSW construction is non-deterministic.")
            faiss.omp_set_num_threads(1)
        # Determine metric
        if distance == 'euclidean':
            index = faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(dim, 32, faiss.METRIC_L2)
            index.add(X)
            tree = index
            D, I = index.search(X, k)
            nbrs = I[:, 1:].astype(np.int32)
            knn_distances = np.sqrt(D[:, 1:]).astype(np.float32)

        elif distance == 'angular':
            index = faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(dim, 32, faiss.METRIC_INNER_PRODUCT)
            X_norm = X.copy()
            faiss.normalize_L2(X_norm)
            index.add(X_norm)
            tree = index

            D, I = index.search(X_norm, k)
            nbrs = I[:, 1:].astype(np.int32)
            D = np.minimum(D[:, 1:], 1.0)
            knn_distances = np.sqrt(2 * (1 - D)).astype(np.float32)

        elif distance == 'manhattan':
            index = faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(dim, 32, faiss.METRIC_L1)
            index.add(X)
            tree = index

            D, I = index.search(X, k)
            nbrs = I[:, 1:].astype(np.int32)
            knn_distances = D[:, 1:].astype(np.float32)
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(f"Faiss backend does not support {distance} metric in this implementation.")

    elif knn_backend == 'voyager':
        if voyager is None:
            raise ImportError("voyager is not installed.")

        if random_state is not None:
            logger.warning("Using Voyager with random state: Determinism depends on the underlying Voyager implementation.")
        if distance == 'euclidean':
            metric = voyager.Space.Euclidean
        elif distance == 'angular':
            metric = voyager.Space.Cosine
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(f"Voyager backend does not support {distance} metric in this implementation.")

        # Voyager accepts random_seed for deterministic behavior
        index = voyager.Index(metric, num_dimensions=dim, random_seed=random_state if random_state is not None else 1)
        index.add_items(X, num_threads=1)
        tree = index

        # Query
        I, D = index.query(X, k=k)

        # Exclude self
        nbrs = I[:, 1:].astype(np.int32)
        knn_distances = np.sqrt(D[:, 1:]).astype(np.float32)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown knn_backend: {knn_backend}")

    return tree, nbrs, knn_distances


def generate_pair(
        X,
        n_neighbors,
        n_MN,
        n_FP,
        distance='euclidean',
        verbose=True,
        knn_backend='faiss'
):
    '''Generate pairs for the dataset.
    '''
    n, dim = X.shape
    # sample more neighbors than needed
    if n - 1 < n_neighbors:
        logger.warning("Sample size is smaller than number of neighbor pairs"
                       " requested. n_neighbors will be reduced.")
    n_neighbors_extra = min(n_neighbors + 50, n - 1)
    n_neighbors = min(n_neighbors, n - 1)
    if n - 1 < n_FP:
        logger.warning("Sample size is smaller than number of further pairs"
                       " requested. n_FP will be reduced.")
    n_FP = min(n_FP, n - 1)
    if n - 1 < n_MN:
        logger.warning("Sample size is smaller than number of mid-near pairs"
                       " requested. n_MN will be reduced.")
    n_MN = min(n_MN, n - 1)
    if n_neighbors + n_MN + n_FP >= n:
        logger.warning("Sample size is smaller than the total number of assigned points, n_neighbors, n_MN, n_FP would be reorganized")

        # Infer ratios from the inputs provided
        if n_neighbors > 0:
            mn_r = n_MN / n_neighbors
            fp_r = n_FP / n_neighbors

            n_neighbors = int(n / (1 + mn_r + fp_r))
            n_MN = int(n_neighbors * mn_r)
            n_FP = int(n_neighbors * fp_r)
        else:
            # Edge case handling
            n_neighbors = 0
            n_MN = 0
            n_FP = 0

    tree, nbrs, knn_distances = compute_nearest_neighbors(
        X, n_neighbors_extra, distance, knn_backend, _RANDOM_STATE
    )
    print_verbose("Found nearest neighbor", verbose)
    sig = np.maximum(np.mean(knn_distances[:, 3:6], axis=1), 1e-10)
    print_verbose("Calculated sigma", verbose)
    scaled_dist = scale_dist(knn_distances, sig, nbrs)
    print_verbose("Found scaled dist", verbose)
    pair_neighbors = sample_neighbors_pair(X, scaled_dist, nbrs, n_neighbors)

    option = distance_to_option(distance=distance)

    if _RANDOM_STATE is None:
        pair_MN = sample_MN_pair(X, n_MN, option)
        pair_FP = sample_FP_pair(X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP)
    else:
        pair_MN = sample_MN_pair_deterministic(X, n_MN, _RANDOM_STATE, option)
        pair_FP = sample_FP_pair_deterministic(
            X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP, _RANDOM_STATE)
    return pair_neighbors, pair_MN, pair_FP, tree


def generate_pair_no_neighbors(
        X,
        n_neighbors,
        n_MN,
        n_FP,
        pair_neighbors,
        distance='euclidean',
        verbose=True
):
    '''Generate mid-near pairs and further pairs for a given dataset.
    This function is useful when the nearest neighbors comes from a given set.
    '''
    option = distance_to_option(distance=distance)
    
    if _RANDOM_STATE is None:
        pair_MN = sample_MN_pair(X, n_MN, option)
        pair_FP = sample_FP_pair(X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP)
    else:
        pair_MN = sample_MN_pair_deterministic(X, n_MN, _RANDOM_STATE, option)
        pair_FP = sample_FP_pair_deterministic(
            X, pair_neighbors, n_neighbors, n_FP, _RANDOM_STATE)
    return pair_neighbors, pair_MN, pair_FP


def pacmap(
        X,
        n_dims,
        pair_neighbors,
        pair_MN,
        pair_FP,
        lr,
        num_iters,
        Yinit,
        verbose,
        intermediate,
        inter_snapshots,
        pca_solution,
        tsvd=None
):
    start_time = time.time()
    n, _ = X.shape

    if intermediate:
        intermediate_states = np.empty(
            (len(inter_snapshots), n, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)
    else:
        intermediate_states = None
    # Initialize the embedding
    if isinstance(Yinit, np.ndarray):
        Yinit = Yinit.astype(np.float32)
        scaler = preprocessing.StandardScaler().fit(Yinit)
        Y = scaler.transform(Yinit) * 0.0001
    elif Yinit is None or (isinstance(Yinit, str) and Yinit == "pca"):
        if pca_solution:
            Y = 0.01 * X[:, :n_dims]
        else:
            Y = 0.01 * tsvd.transform(X).astype(np.float32)
    elif (isinstance(Yinit, str) and Yinit == "random"):  # random or hamming distance
        if _RANDOM_STATE is not None:
            np.random.seed(_RANDOM_STATE)
        Y = np.random.normal(size=[n, n_dims]).astype(np.float32) * 0.0001
    else:
        raise ValueError((f"The argument init is of the type {type(Yinit)}. "
                          "Currently, PaCMAP only supports user supplied "
                          "numpy.ndarray object as input, or one of "
                          "['pca', 'random']."))
    # Initialize parameters for optimizer
    w_MN_init = 1000.
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    m = np.zeros_like(Y, dtype=np.float32)
    v = np.zeros_like(Y, dtype=np.float32)

    if intermediate and inter_snapshots[0] == 0:
        itr_ind = 1  # move counter to one step
        intermediate_states[0, :, :] = Y

    print_verbose(
        (pair_neighbors.shape, pair_MN.shape, pair_FP.shape), verbose)

    num_iters_total = sum(num_iters)
    for itr in range(num_iters_total):
        w_MN, w_neighbors, w_FP = find_weight(w_MN_init, itr, num_iters=num_iters)

        grad = pacmap_grad(Y, pair_neighbors, pair_MN,
                           pair_FP, w_neighbors, w_MN, w_FP)
        C = grad[-1, 0]
        if verbose and itr == 0:
            print(f"Initial Loss: {C}")
        update_embedding_adam(Y, grad, m, v, beta1, beta2, lr, itr)
        if intermediate:
            if (itr + 1) == inter_snapshots[itr_ind]:
                intermediate_states[itr_ind, :, :] = Y
                itr_ind += 1
        if (itr + 1) % 10 == 0:
            print_verbose("Iteration: %4d, Loss: %f" % (itr + 1, C), verbose)

    elapsed = time.time() - start_time
    print_verbose(f"Elapsed time: {elapsed:.2f}s", verbose)

    return Y, intermediate_states, pair_neighbors, pair_MN, pair_FP

What is copied unchanged

Only the short file-level provenance note and the reduced import list were added
so the focused extract can stand alone. No copied function body was edited.

What is new and not claimed as original PaCMAP
----------------------------------------------
All new work is isolated in ../sc_pacmap.py:

1. Three-mode if/elif selector.
2. Input validation.
3. Mode 2 concatenation of features and separately standardized XYZ.
4. PCA-Geary spatial-continuity calculations.
5. Automatic k_spatial / k_attribute allocation.
6. Construction of the replacement nearest-neighbour pair matrix.
7. Verification functions comparing:
   - copied PaCMAP against installed pacmap==0.9.1;
   - SC disabled against copied ordinary PaCMAP.

SC-PaCMAP changes only pair_neighbors. After that matrix is constructed,
original generate_pair_no_neighbors() creates the mid-near and further pairs,
and original pacmap() performs initialization, all three loss phases, gradients,
and Adam optimization.

================================================================================
FILE: code/sc_pacmap/sc_pacmap.py
================================================================================
"""Three PaCMAP modes using an untouched PaCMAP 0.9.1 core.

Inputs
------
features_standardized : n x p standardized feature matrix
coordinates_m        : n x 2 or n x 3 coordinates in metres

Modes
-----
1. ``pacmap_features``
   Original PaCMAP. Input = features only.

2. ``pacmap_features_xyz``
   Original PaCMAP again. Input = features plus separately standardized XYZ.
   The PaCMAP code is identical to mode 1; only the input matrix changes.

3. ``sc_pacmap``
    Keep the complete original feature-neighbour graph ``G_F`` and construct a
    separate PCA-Geary spatial graph ``G_S`` from geographic candidates. Add one
    weighted SC attraction only in Phase 3. Its target attraction share is
    calculated once from Geary lambda and the unresolved NN/SC attraction gap,
    then ramped in while the same share is transferred out of NN attraction.

The official copied functions are in ``pacmap_original/pacmap_core_reference.py``.
Their signatures and function bodies are not edited. Search this wrapper for
``SC INJECTION`` to see every added algorithm block.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version
import math
from typing import Any

import numba
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

from pacmap_original import pacmap_core_reference as original


MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES = "pacmap_features"
MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES_XYZ = "pacmap_features_xyz"
MODE_SC_PACMAP = "sc_pacmap"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PaCMAPSettings:
    """Settings shared by copied PaCMAP, library PaCMAP and SC-PaCMAP."""

    n_components: int = 2
    n_neighbors: int | None = 15
    mn_ratio: float = 0.5
    fp_ratio: float = 2.0
    learning_rate: float = 1.0
    num_iters: tuple[int, int, int] = (100, 100, 250)
    random_state: int = 42
    knn_backend: str = "faiss"
    verbose: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SCPaCMAPConfig:
    """Settings for the separate Phase-3 spatial-continuity injection."""

    c_sc: float = 10.0
    phase3_ramp_iterations: int = 25
    overlap_sc_factor: float = 0.5
    max_target_share: float = 0.95


@dataclass
class PaCMAPResult:
    """Embedding and pair arrays returned by one run."""

    embedding: np.ndarray
    pair_neighbors: np.ndarray
    pair_mn: np.ndarray
    pair_fp: np.ndarray
    diagnostics: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass
class SCDiagnostics:
    """Graph, weighting, and Phase-3 diagnostics for the SC injection."""

    geary_c: np.ndarray
    continuity_weight: np.ndarray
    spatial_strength_lambda: float
    k_spatial: int
    k_attribute: int
    ordinary_neighbors: np.ndarray
    selected_spatial_neighbors: np.ndarray
    final_neighbors: np.ndarray
    pair_sc: np.ndarray
    sc_distance: np.ndarray
    sc_edge_weight: np.ndarray
    overlap_mask: np.ndarray
    automatic_target_share: float
    overlap_fraction: float
    sc_distance_scale: float
    mean_sc_weight_before_overlap: float
    mean_sc_weight_after_overlap: float
    mean_realized_sc_share_active: float
    mean_w_sc_active: float
    max_w_sc_active: float
    c_sc: float
    phase3_ramp_iterations: int


@dataclass
class LegacySCDiagnostics:
    """Diagnostics for the superseded neighbour-replacement benchmark only."""

    geary_c: np.ndarray
    continuity_weight: np.ndarray
    spatial_strength_lambda: float
    k_spatial: int
    k_attribute: int
    ordinary_neighbors: np.ndarray
    selected_spatial_neighbors: np.ndarray
    final_neighbors: np.ndarray


@dataclass
class _SCGraph:
    """Internal data for the separate spatial-continuity graph."""

    ordinary_neighbors: np.ndarray
    selected_spatial_neighbors: np.ndarray
    pair_sc: np.ndarray
    sc_distance: np.ndarray
    overlap_mask: np.ndarray
    geary_c: np.ndarray
    continuity_weight: np.ndarray
    spatial_strength_lambda: float


def _as_features(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Validate a feature matrix without silently filling missing values."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[0] < 2 or x.shape[1] < 1:
        raise ValueError("features must be a two-dimensional non-empty matrix")
    if not np.isfinite(x).all():
        raise ValueError("features contain missing or non-finite values")
    return np.ascontiguousarray(x)


def _as_coordinates(xyz: np.ndarray, n_rows: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Validate X/Y or X/Y/Z coordinates."""
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=np.float32)
    if xyz.ndim != 2 or xyz.shape[0] != n_rows:
        raise ValueError("coordinates must have one row per feature row")
    if xyz.shape[1] not in (2, 3):
        raise ValueError("coordinates must contain X/Y or X/Y/Z")
    if not np.isfinite(xyz).all():
        raise ValueError("coordinates contain missing or non-finite values")
    return np.ascontiguousarray(xyz)


def _decide_pair_counts(
    n_samples: int,
    n_neighbors: int | None,
    mn_ratio: float,
    fp_ratio: float,
) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """Match PaCMAP 0.9.1 ``PaCMAP.decide_num_pairs``."""
    if n_neighbors is None:
        if n_samples <= 10000:
            n_neighbors = 10
        else:
            n_neighbors = int(
                round(10 + 15 * (np.log10(n_samples) - 4))
            )

    n_mn = int(round(n_neighbors * mn_ratio))
    n_fp = int(round(n_neighbors * fp_ratio))
    n_neighbors = min(n_neighbors, n_samples - 1)
    n_fp = min(n_fp, n_samples - 1 - n_neighbors)
    n_mn = min(n_mn, n_samples - 1)

    if n_neighbors + n_mn + n_fp >= n_samples:
        n_neighbors = int(n_samples / (1 + mn_ratio + fp_ratio))
        n_mn = int(
            n_samples / (1 + mn_ratio + fp_ratio) * mn_ratio
        )
        n_fp = int(
            n_samples / (1 + mn_ratio + fp_ratio) * fp_ratio
        )

    if n_neighbors < 1:
        raise ValueError("The number of nearest neighbors cannot be below 1")
    if n_fp < 1:
        raise ValueError("The number of further points cannot be below 1")
    return n_neighbors, n_mn, n_fp


def _preprocess_with_original(
    x: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, bool, Any]:
    """Call the copied original ``preprocess_X`` function unchanged."""
    x = np.copy(x).astype(np.float32)
    x_pre, pca_solution, transformer, _, _, _ = original.preprocess_X(
        x,
        "euclidean",
        True,
        settings.verbose,
        settings.random_state,
        x.shape[1],
        settings.n_components,
    )
    return np.ascontiguousarray(x_pre, dtype=np.float32), pca_solution, transformer


def _optimize_with_original(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    pca_solution: bool,
    transformer: Any,
    pair_neighbors: np.ndarray,
    pair_mn: np.ndarray,
    pair_fp: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Call the copied original ``pacmap`` optimizer unchanged."""
    y, _, _, _, _ = original.pacmap(
        x_preprocessed,
        settings.n_components,
        np.ascontiguousarray(pair_neighbors, dtype=np.int32),
        np.ascontiguousarray(pair_mn, dtype=np.int32),
        np.ascontiguousarray(pair_fp, dtype=np.int32),
        settings.learning_rate,
        settings.num_iters,
        "pca",
        settings.verbose,
        False,
        [0, 10, 30, 60, 100, 120, 140, 170, 200, 250, 300, 350, 450],
        pca_solution,
        transformer,
    )
    return np.ascontiguousarray(y, dtype=np.float32)


def run_copied_original_pacmap(
    x_input: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
) -> PaCMAPResult:
    """Run the copied PaCMAP 0.9.1 path with no SC modification."""
    x_input = _as_features(x_input)
    n_neighbors, n_mn, n_fp = _decide_pair_counts(
        len(x_input),
        settings.n_neighbors,
        settings.mn_ratio,
        settings.fp_ratio,
    )

    # The official class writes the seed to this module-level variable.
    original._RANDOM_STATE = settings.random_state

    x_pre, pca_solution, transformer = _preprocess_with_original(
        x_input, settings
    )
    pair_neighbors, pair_mn, pair_fp, _ = original.generate_pair(
        x_pre,
        n_neighbors,
        n_mn,
        n_fp,
        "euclidean",
        settings.verbose,
        settings.knn_backend,
    )
    embedding = _optimize_with_original(
        x_pre,
        pca_solution,
        transformer,
        pair_neighbors,
        pair_mn,
        pair_fp,
        settings,
    )
    return PaCMAPResult(
        embedding=embedding,
        pair_neighbors=pair_neighbors,
        pair_mn=pair_mn,
        pair_fp=pair_fp,
        diagnostics={
            "mode": "copied_original_pacmap",
            "n_neighbors": n_neighbors,
            "n_mn": n_mn,
            "n_fp": n_fp,
        },
    )


def _geary_c_by_component(
    standardized_scores: np.ndarray,
    geographic_neighbors: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Directed-kNN Geary's C for each standardized PCA component."""
    n = standardized_scores.shape[0]
    w0 = float(n * geographic_neighbors.shape[1])
    centered = standardized_scores - standardized_scores.mean(
        axis=0, keepdims=True
    )
    denominator = np.sum(centered * centered, axis=0)
    differences = (
        standardized_scores[:, None, :]
        - standardized_scores[geographic_neighbors, :]
    )
    numerator = np.sum(differences * differences, axis=(0, 1))
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        geary_c = (n - 1.0) * numerator / (2.0 * w0 * denominator)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(geary_c), geary_c, 1.0)


@numba.njit(cache=True, fastmath=True)
def _continuity_distance(
    standardized_scores: np.ndarray,
    geographic_neighbors: np.ndarray,
    component_weights: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Weighted PCA distance for geographic candidate neighbours."""
    n, k = geographic_neighbors.shape
    q = standardized_scores.shape[1]
    output = np.empty((n, k), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        for position in range(k):
            j = geographic_neighbors[i, position]
            value = 0.0
            for component in range(q):
                difference = (
                    standardized_scores[i, component]
                    - standardized_scores[j, component]
                )
                value += (
                    component_weights[component]
                    * difference
                    * difference
                )
            output[i, position] = value
    return output


def _neighbors_to_pairs(neighbors: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Convert an n-by-k neighbour matrix to PaCMAP's pair format."""
    n, k = neighbors.shape
    pairs = np.empty((n * k, 2), dtype=np.int32)
    pairs[:, 0] = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), k)
    pairs[:, 1] = neighbors.reshape(-1)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(pairs)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NEIGHBOUR-REPLACEMENT-GRAPH BENCHMARK ONLY
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _attribute_neighbor_ranking(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    n_neighbors: int,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Reproduce the ordinary locally scaled PaCMAP neighbour ranking."""
    n_search = min(n_neighbors + 50, len(x_preprocessed) - 1)
    _, neighbors, distances = original.compute_nearest_neighbors(
        x_preprocessed,
        n_search,
        "euclidean",
        settings.knn_backend,
        settings.random_state,
    )
    sigma = np.maximum(np.mean(distances[:, 3:6], axis=1), 1e-10)
    scaled_distance = original.scale_dist(distances, sigma, neighbors)
    ranked_pairs = original.sample_neighbors_pair(
        x_preprocessed,
        scaled_distance,
        neighbors,
        n_search,
    )
    return np.ascontiguousarray(
        ranked_pairs[:, 1].reshape(len(x_preprocessed), n_search),
        dtype=np.int32,
    )


@numba.njit(cache=True)
def _combine_legacy_neighbors(
    spatial_ranking: np.ndarray,
    k_spatial: int,
    attribute_ranking: np.ndarray,
    k_total: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Fill a fixed-size graph with SC neighbours first, then feature NN."""
    n = attribute_ranking.shape[0]
    output = np.empty((n, k_total), dtype=np.int32)
    for i in range(n):
        count = 0
        for position in range(k_spatial):
            candidate = spatial_ranking[i, position]
            duplicate = False
            for previous in range(count):
                if output[i, previous] == candidate:
                    duplicate = True
                    break
            if not duplicate:
                output[i, count] = candidate
                count += 1

        for position in range(attribute_ranking.shape[1]):
            if count == k_total:
                break
            candidate = attribute_ranking[i, position]
            duplicate = False
            for previous in range(count):
                if output[i, previous] == candidate:
                    duplicate = True
                    break
            if not duplicate:
                output[i, count] = candidate
                count += 1
    return output


def _construct_legacy_sc_pairs(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    n_neighbors: int,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, LegacySCDiagnostics]:
    """Construct the superseded mixed NN graph used as the red baseline."""
    attribute_ranking = _attribute_neighbor_ranking(
        x_preprocessed, n_neighbors, settings
    )
    ordinary_neighbors = attribute_ranking[:, :n_neighbors].copy()
    _, geographic_neighbors, _ = original.compute_nearest_neighbors(
        np.ascontiguousarray(coordinates_m, dtype=np.float32),
        n_neighbors,
        "euclidean",
        settings.knn_backend,
        settings.random_state,
    )

    n_components = min(
        100, x_preprocessed.shape[1], x_preprocessed.shape[0] - 1
    )
    pca = PCA(n_components=n_components, random_state=settings.random_state)
    scores = pca.fit_transform(x_preprocessed).astype(np.float64)
    score_std = np.std(scores, axis=0, ddof=0)
    score_std = np.where(score_std > 0.0, score_std, 1.0)
    standardized_scores = (
        scores - np.mean(scores, axis=0, keepdims=True)
    ) / score_std
    geary_c = _geary_c_by_component(
        standardized_scores, geographic_neighbors
    )
    signed_continuity = np.clip(1.0 - geary_c, -1.0, 1.0)
    continuity_weight = np.clip(signed_continuity, 0.0, 1.0)
    spatial_strength = float(
        np.clip(
            np.sum(
                pca.explained_variance_ratio_.astype(np.float64)
                * signed_continuity
            ),
            0.0,
            1.0,
        )
    )
    k_spatial = int(np.round(n_neighbors * spatial_strength))
    k_attribute = n_neighbors - k_spatial
    component_weights = (
        pca.explained_variance_.astype(np.float64) * continuity_weight
    )
    continuity_distance = _continuity_distance(
        np.ascontiguousarray(standardized_scores),
        np.ascontiguousarray(geographic_neighbors, dtype=np.int32),
        np.ascontiguousarray(component_weights),
    )
    spatial_order = np.argsort(
        continuity_distance, axis=1, kind="stable"
    )
    spatial_ranking = np.take_along_axis(
        geographic_neighbors, spatial_order, axis=1
    ).astype(np.int32)
    selected_spatial = spatial_ranking[:, :k_spatial].copy()
    final_neighbors = _combine_legacy_neighbors(
        np.ascontiguousarray(spatial_ranking),
        k_spatial,
        np.ascontiguousarray(attribute_ranking),
        n_neighbors,
    )
    diagnostics = LegacySCDiagnostics(
        geary_c=geary_c,
        continuity_weight=continuity_weight,
        spatial_strength_lambda=spatial_strength,
        k_spatial=k_spatial,
        k_attribute=k_attribute,
        ordinary_neighbors=ordinary_neighbors,
        selected_spatial_neighbors=selected_spatial,
        final_neighbors=final_neighbors,
    )
    return _neighbors_to_pairs(final_neighbors), diagnostics


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SC INJECTION 1: construct a separate PCA-Geary graph G_S
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _construct_separate_sc_graph(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    pair_feature: np.ndarray,
    n_neighbors: int,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
) -> _SCGraph:
    """Keep original G_F complete and build an additional SC graph."""
    ordinary_neighbors = np.ascontiguousarray(
        pair_feature[:, 1].reshape(len(x_preprocessed), n_neighbors),
        dtype=np.int32,
    )

    _, geographic_neighbors, _ = original.compute_nearest_neighbors(
        np.ascontiguousarray(coordinates_m, dtype=np.float32),
        n_neighbors,
        "euclidean",
        settings.knn_backend,
        settings.random_state,
    )

    n_components = min(
        100,
        x_preprocessed.shape[1],
        x_preprocessed.shape[0] - 1,
    )
    pca = PCA(n_components=n_components, random_state=settings.random_state)
    scores = pca.fit_transform(x_preprocessed).astype(np.float64)
    score_std = np.std(scores, axis=0, ddof=0)
    score_std = np.where(score_std > 0.0, score_std, 1.0)
    standardized_scores = (
        scores - np.mean(scores, axis=0, keepdims=True)
    ) / score_std

    geary_c = _geary_c_by_component(
        standardized_scores, geographic_neighbors
    )

    signed_continuity = np.clip(1.0 - geary_c, -1.0, 1.0)
    continuity_weight = np.clip(signed_continuity, 0.0, 1.0)
    spatial_strength = float(
        np.clip(
            np.sum(
                pca.explained_variance_ratio_.astype(np.float64)
                * signed_continuity
            ),
            0.0,
            1.0,
        )
    )
    k_spatial = int(np.round(n_neighbors * spatial_strength))

    if k_spatial == 0:
        selected_spatial = np.empty(
            (len(x_preprocessed), 0), dtype=np.int32
        )
        pair_sc = np.empty((0, 2), dtype=np.int32)
        selected_distance = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float32)
    else:
        component_weights = (
            pca.explained_variance_.astype(np.float64) * continuity_weight
        )
        continuity_distance = _continuity_distance(
            np.ascontiguousarray(standardized_scores),
            np.ascontiguousarray(geographic_neighbors, dtype=np.int32),
            np.ascontiguousarray(component_weights),
        )
        spatial_order = np.argsort(
            continuity_distance, axis=1, kind="stable"
        )[:, :k_spatial]
        selected_spatial = np.take_along_axis(
            geographic_neighbors, spatial_order, axis=1
        ).astype(np.int32)
        selected_distance = np.take_along_axis(
            continuity_distance, spatial_order, axis=1
        ).reshape(-1).astype(np.float32)
        pair_sc = _neighbors_to_pairs(selected_spatial)

    feature_pair_set = {
        (int(i), int(j)) for i, j in np.asarray(pair_feature, dtype=np.int32)
    }
    overlap_mask = np.fromiter(
        ((int(i), int(j)) in feature_pair_set for i, j in pair_sc),
        dtype=np.bool_,
        count=len(pair_sc),
    )

    return _SCGraph(
        ordinary_neighbors=ordinary_neighbors,
        selected_spatial_neighbors=np.ascontiguousarray(selected_spatial),
        pair_sc=np.ascontiguousarray(pair_sc, dtype=np.int32),
        sc_distance=np.ascontiguousarray(selected_distance, dtype=np.float32),
        overlap_mask=np.ascontiguousarray(overlap_mask),
        geary_c=np.asarray(geary_c, dtype=np.float64),
        continuity_weight=np.asarray(continuity_weight, dtype=np.float64),
        spatial_strength_lambda=spatial_strength,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SC INJECTION 2: convert direct D_SC to edge confidence
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _make_sc_edge_weights(
    sc_distance: np.ndarray,
    overlap_mask: np.ndarray,
    overlap_factor: float,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float, float, float]:
    """Give smaller D_SC stronger attraction, then downweight overlap."""
    if len(sc_distance) == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float32), 1.0, 0.0, 0.0

    positive = sc_distance[sc_distance > 0.0]
    scale = float(np.median(positive)) if len(positive) else 1.0
    scale = max(scale, 1e-12)
    raw_weight = 1.0 / (1.0 + sc_distance.astype(np.float64) / scale)
    raw_weight /= max(float(np.mean(raw_weight)), 1e-12)
    mean_before_overlap = float(np.mean(raw_weight))

    final_weight = raw_weight.astype(np.float32)
    final_weight[overlap_mask] *= np.float32(overlap_factor)
    return (
        np.ascontiguousarray(final_weight),
        scale,
        mean_before_overlap,
        float(np.mean(final_weight)),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SC INJECTION 3: calculate the Phase-3 attraction-gap share once
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@numba.njit(cache=True, fastmath=True)
def _unit_attraction_sums(
    y: np.ndarray,
    pair_feature: np.ndarray,
    pair_sc: np.ndarray,
    sc_edge_weight: np.ndarray,
    c_sc: float,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Return NN and SC force totals when both graph weights equal one."""
    nn_sum = 0.0
    for edge in range(pair_feature.shape[0]):
        i = pair_feature[edge, 0]
        j = pair_feature[edge, 1]
        squared_distance = 0.0
        for dimension in range(y.shape[1]):
            difference = y[i, dimension] - y[j, dimension]
            squared_distance += difference * difference
        d_tilde = 1.0 + squared_distance
        nn_sum += (
            20.0
            * math.sqrt(squared_distance)
            / ((10.0 + d_tilde) * (10.0 + d_tilde))
        )

    sc_sum = 0.0
    for edge in range(pair_sc.shape[0]):
        i = pair_sc[edge, 0]
        j = pair_sc[edge, 1]
        squared_distance = 0.0
        for dimension in range(y.shape[1]):
            difference = y[i, dimension] - y[j, dimension]
            squared_distance += difference * difference
        d_tilde = 1.0 + squared_distance
        sc_sum += (
            sc_edge_weight[edge]
            * (2.0 * c_sc)
            * math.sqrt(squared_distance)
            / ((c_sc + d_tilde) * (c_sc + d_tilde))
        )
    return nn_sum, sc_sum


def _automatic_target_share(
    spatial_strength_lambda: float,
    unit_nn_total: float,
    unit_sc_total: float,
    n_nn_edges: int,
    effective_sc_edge_count: float,
    maximum: float,
) -> float:
    """Convert lambda and the unresolved attraction gap to an SC share."""
    lam = float(np.clip(spatial_strength_lambda, 0.0, maximum))
    if unit_sc_total <= 1e-20 or effective_sc_edge_count <= 1e-20:
        return 0.0
    if unit_nn_total <= 1e-20:
        return lam

    mean_nn = unit_nn_total / max(float(n_nn_edges), 1.0)
    mean_sc = unit_sc_total / max(float(effective_sc_edge_count), 1e-12)
    numerator = lam * mean_sc
    denominator = numerator + (1.0 - lam) * mean_nn
    if denominator <= 1e-20:
        return lam
    return float(np.clip(numerator / denominator, 0.0, maximum))


def _phase3_ramp(
    iteration: int,
    num_iters: tuple[int, int, int],
    ramp_iterations: int,
) -> float:
    """Return zero before Phase 3, then linearly ramp to one."""
    phase3_start = num_iters[0] + num_iters[1]
    if iteration < phase3_start:
        return 0.0
    if ramp_iterations <= 0:
        return 1.0
    return float(
        np.clip((iteration - phase3_start) / ramp_iterations, 0.0, 1.0)
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SC INJECTION 4: append one SC loss gradient to original.pacmap_grad
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@numba.njit(cache=True, fastmath=True)
def _add_sc_gradient(
    gradient: np.ndarray,
    y: np.ndarray,
    pair_sc: np.ndarray,
    sc_edge_weight: np.ndarray,
    w_sc: float,
    c_sc: float,
) -> None:
    """Add L_SC while leaving original NN/MN/FP loss code untouched."""
    sc_loss = 0.0
    for edge in range(pair_sc.shape[0]):
        i = pair_sc[edge, 0]
        j = pair_sc[edge, 1]
        d_tilde = 1.0
        for dimension in range(y.shape[1]):
            difference = y[i, dimension] - y[j, dimension]
            d_tilde += difference * difference

        edge_coefficient = w_sc * sc_edge_weight[edge]
        sc_loss += edge_coefficient * d_tilde / (c_sc + d_tilde)
        gradient_coefficient = (
            edge_coefficient * (2.0 * c_sc) / ((c_sc + d_tilde) ** 2)
        )
        for dimension in range(y.shape[1]):
            difference = y[i, dimension] - y[j, dimension]
            gradient[i, dimension] += gradient_coefficient * difference
            gradient[j, dimension] -= gradient_coefficient * difference
    gradient[-1, 0] += sc_loss


def _initialize_embedding(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    pca_solution: bool,
    transformer: Any,
    n_components: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Repeat only the copied optimizer's PCA initialization expression."""
    if pca_solution:
        embedding = 0.01 * x_preprocessed[:, :n_components]
    else:
        embedding = 0.01 * transformer.transform(
            x_preprocessed
        ).astype(np.float32)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(embedding, dtype=np.float32)


def _optimize_with_sc_injection(
    x_preprocessed: np.ndarray,
    pca_solution: bool,
    transformer: Any,
    pair_feature: np.ndarray,
    pair_mn: np.ndarray,
    pair_fp: np.ndarray,
    sc_graph: _SCGraph,
    sc_edge_weight: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings,
    config: SCPaCMAPConfig,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float, float, float, float]:
    """Run original PaCMAP with a separate SC attraction only in Phase 3."""
    embedding = _initialize_embedding(
        x_preprocessed,
        pca_solution,
        transformer,
        settings.n_components,
    )
    first_moment = np.zeros_like(embedding, dtype=np.float32)
    second_moment = np.zeros_like(embedding, dtype=np.float32)
    phase3_start = settings.num_iters[0] + settings.num_iters[1]
    target_share: float | None = None
    effective_sc_edge_count = float(np.sum(sc_edge_weight))
    active_realized_shares: list[float] = []
    active_w_sc: list[float] = []

    for iteration in range(sum(settings.num_iters)):
        w_mn, w_nn_original, w_fp = original.find_weight(
            1000.0,
            iteration,
            num_iters=settings.num_iters,
        )

        if iteration < phase3_start:
            w_feature = float(w_nn_original)
            w_sc = 0.0
            realized_share = 0.0
        else:
            unit_nn, unit_sc = _unit_attraction_sums(
                embedding,
                pair_feature,
                sc_graph.pair_sc,
                sc_edge_weight,
                float(config.c_sc),
            )
            if target_share is None:
                target_share = _automatic_target_share(
                    sc_graph.spatial_strength_lambda,
                    unit_nn,
                    unit_sc,
                    len(pair_feature),
                    effective_sc_edge_count,
                    config.max_target_share,
                )

            ramp = _phase3_ramp(
                iteration,
                settings.num_iters,
                config.phase3_ramp_iterations,
            )
            scheduled_share = float(
                np.clip(target_share * ramp, 0.0, config.max_target_share)
            )

            # Transfer the scheduled share out of NN attraction into G_S.
            w_feature = float(w_nn_original * (1.0 - scheduled_share))
            if scheduled_share > 0.0 and unit_sc > 1e-20:
                w_sc = float(
                    w_nn_original
                    * scheduled_share
                    * unit_nn
                    / unit_sc
                )
                realized_share = float(
                    (w_sc * unit_sc)
                    / max(w_feature * unit_nn + w_sc * unit_sc, 1e-20)
                )
            else:
                w_sc = 0.0
                realized_share = 0.0

        # ORIGINAL NN/MN/FP loss and gradient are called, not reimplemented.
        gradient = original.pacmap_grad(
            embedding,
            pair_feature,
            pair_mn,
            pair_fp,
            float(w_feature),
            float(w_mn),
            float(w_fp),
        )
        if w_sc > 0.0:
            _add_sc_gradient(
                gradient,
                embedding,
                sc_graph.pair_sc,
                sc_edge_weight,
                float(w_sc),
                float(config.c_sc),
            )
        original.update_embedding_adam(
            embedding,
            gradient,
            first_moment,
            second_moment,
            0.9,
            0.999,
            settings.learning_rate,
            iteration,
        )

        if iteration >= phase3_start and w_sc > 0.0:
            active_realized_shares.append(realized_share)
            active_w_sc.append(w_sc)

        if settings.verbose and (iteration + 1) % 10 == 0:
            print(
                f"Iteration {iteration + 1:4d} | "
                f"loss={gradient[-1, 0]:.6f} | "
                f"target={0.0 if target_share is None else target_share:.3f} | "
                f"w_sc={w_sc:.3f}"
            )

    return (
        np.ascontiguousarray(embedding, dtype=np.float32),
        0.0 if target_share is None else float(target_share),
        float(np.mean(active_realized_shares))
        if active_realized_shares
        else 0.0,
        float(np.mean(active_w_sc)) if active_w_sc else 0.0,
        float(np.max(active_w_sc)) if active_w_sc else 0.0,
    )


def run_legacy_sc_pacmap(
    features_standardized: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
) -> tuple[PaCMAPResult, LegacySCDiagnostics]:
    """Run SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph) as a benchmark baseline."""
    features = _as_features(features_standardized)
    coordinates = _as_coordinates(coordinates_m, len(features))
    n_neighbors, n_mn, n_fp = _decide_pair_counts(
        len(features),
        settings.n_neighbors,
        settings.mn_ratio,
        settings.fp_ratio,
    )
    original._RANDOM_STATE = settings.random_state
    x_pre, pca_solution, transformer = _preprocess_with_original(
        features, settings
    )
    pair_neighbors, diagnostics = _construct_legacy_sc_pairs(
        x_pre,
        coordinates,
        n_neighbors,
        settings,
    )
    pair_neighbors, pair_mn, pair_fp = original.generate_pair_no_neighbors(
        x_pre,
        n_neighbors,
        n_mn,
        n_fp,
        pair_neighbors,
        "euclidean",
        settings.verbose,
    )
    embedding = _optimize_with_original(
        x_pre,
        pca_solution,
        transformer,
        pair_neighbors,
        pair_mn,
        pair_fp,
        settings,
    )
    return PaCMAPResult(
        embedding=embedding,
        pair_neighbors=pair_neighbors,
        pair_mn=pair_mn,
        pair_fp=pair_fp,
        diagnostics={
            "mode": "legacy_sc_pacmap_benchmark",
            "n_neighbors": n_neighbors,
            "n_mn": n_mn,
            "n_fp": n_fp,
        },
    ), diagnostics


def run_sc_pacmap(
    features_standardized: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
    *,
    sc_enabled: bool = True,
    config: SCPaCMAPConfig = SCPaCMAPConfig(),
) -> tuple[PaCMAPResult, SCDiagnostics | None]:
    """Run SC-PaCMAP (full graph) with a separate spatial loss."""
    features = _as_features(features_standardized)
    coordinates = _as_coordinates(coordinates_m, len(features))

    if not sc_enabled:
        return run_copied_original_pacmap(features, settings), None

    n_neighbors, n_mn, n_fp = _decide_pair_counts(
        len(features),
        settings.n_neighbors,
        settings.mn_ratio,
        settings.fp_ratio,
    )
    original._RANDOM_STATE = settings.random_state
    x_pre, pca_solution, transformer = _preprocess_with_original(
        features, settings
    )

    # ORIGINAL: construct and retain the complete feature NN/MN/FP graphs.
    pair_neighbors, pair_mn, pair_fp, _ = original.generate_pair(
        x_pre,
        n_neighbors,
        n_mn,
        n_fp,
        "euclidean",
        settings.verbose,
        settings.knn_backend,
    )

    sc_graph = _construct_separate_sc_graph(
        x_pre,
        coordinates,
        pair_neighbors,
        n_neighbors,
        settings,
    )
    sc_edge_weight, distance_scale, mean_before, mean_after = (
        _make_sc_edge_weights(
            sc_graph.sc_distance,
            sc_graph.overlap_mask,
            config.overlap_sc_factor,
        )
    )

    if len(sc_graph.pair_sc) == 0:
        embedding = _optimize_with_original(
            x_pre,
            pca_solution,
            transformer,
            pair_neighbors,
            pair_mn,
            pair_fp,
            settings,
        )
        target_share = 0.0
        mean_realized_share = 0.0
        mean_w_sc = 0.0
        max_w_sc = 0.0
    else:
        (
            embedding,
            target_share,
            mean_realized_share,
            mean_w_sc,
            max_w_sc,
        ) = _optimize_with_sc_injection(
            x_pre,
            pca_solution,
            transformer,
            pair_neighbors,
            pair_mn,
            pair_fp,
            sc_graph,
            sc_edge_weight,
            settings,
            config,
        )

    sc_diagnostics = SCDiagnostics(
        geary_c=sc_graph.geary_c,
        continuity_weight=sc_graph.continuity_weight,
        spatial_strength_lambda=sc_graph.spatial_strength_lambda,
        k_spatial=sc_graph.selected_spatial_neighbors.shape[1],
        k_attribute=n_neighbors,
        ordinary_neighbors=sc_graph.ordinary_neighbors,
        selected_spatial_neighbors=sc_graph.selected_spatial_neighbors,
        # Kept for compatibility. G_F is no longer replaced or merged.
        final_neighbors=sc_graph.ordinary_neighbors.copy(),
        pair_sc=sc_graph.pair_sc,
        sc_distance=sc_graph.sc_distance,
        sc_edge_weight=sc_edge_weight,
        overlap_mask=sc_graph.overlap_mask,
        automatic_target_share=target_share,
        overlap_fraction=(
            float(np.mean(sc_graph.overlap_mask))
            if len(sc_graph.overlap_mask)
            else 0.0
        ),
        sc_distance_scale=distance_scale,
        mean_sc_weight_before_overlap=mean_before,
        mean_sc_weight_after_overlap=mean_after,
        mean_realized_sc_share_active=mean_realized_share,
        mean_w_sc_active=mean_w_sc,
        max_w_sc_active=max_w_sc,
        c_sc=config.c_sc,
        phase3_ramp_iterations=config.phase3_ramp_iterations,
    )
    return PaCMAPResult(
        embedding=embedding,
        pair_neighbors=pair_neighbors,
        pair_mn=pair_mn,
        pair_fp=pair_fp,
        diagnostics={
            "mode": "sc_pacmap",
            "n_neighbors": n_neighbors,
            "n_mn": n_mn,
            "n_fp": n_fp,
            "n_sc": sc_graph.selected_spatial_neighbors.shape[1],
            "automatic_target_share": target_share,
        },
    ), sc_diagnostics


def fit_transform(
    features_standardized: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
    config: SCPaCMAPConfig = SCPaCMAPConfig(),
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, SCDiagnostics]:
    """Notebook-friendly enhanced runner returning embedding and diagnostics."""
    result, diagnostics = run_sc_pacmap(
        features_standardized,
        coordinates_m,
        settings,
        config=config,
    )
    if diagnostics is None:
        raise RuntimeError("SC diagnostics were not produced")
    return result.embedding, diagnostics


def run_mode(
    mode: str,
    features_standardized: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
) -> tuple[PaCMAPResult, SCDiagnostics | None]:
    """The requested three-mode if/elif selector."""
    features = _as_features(features_standardized)
    coordinates = _as_coordinates(coordinates_m, len(features))

    if mode == MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES:
        # Mode 1: original PaCMAP, features only.
        return run_copied_original_pacmap(features, settings), None

    elif mode == MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES_XYZ:
        # Mode 2: exactly the same original PaCMAP code. Only input changes.
        xyz_standardized = StandardScaler().fit_transform(
            coordinates
        ).astype(np.float32)
        combined_input = np.column_stack(
            [features, xyz_standardized]
        ).astype(np.float32)
        return run_copied_original_pacmap(combined_input, settings), None

    elif mode == MODE_SC_PACMAP:
        # Mode 3: retain G_F and add a separate Phase-3 G_S attraction.
        return run_sc_pacmap(features, coordinates, settings, sc_enabled=True)

    raise ValueError(
        "mode must be 'pacmap_features', 'pacmap_features_xyz' or 'sc_pacmap'"
    )


def verify_sc_disabled_matches_original(
    features_standardized: np.ndarray,
    coordinates_m: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
) -> dict[str, bool | float]:
    """Prove the wrapper is exact when the SC injections are disabled."""
    ordinary = run_copied_original_pacmap(features_standardized, settings)
    disabled, _ = run_sc_pacmap(
        features_standardized,
        coordinates_m,
        settings,
        sc_enabled=False,
    )
    return {
        "neighbor_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            ordinary.pair_neighbors, disabled.pair_neighbors
        ),
        "mid_near_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            ordinary.pair_mn, disabled.pair_mn
        ),
        "further_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            ordinary.pair_fp, disabled.pair_fp
        ),
        "embedding_equal": np.array_equal(
            ordinary.embedding, disabled.embedding
        ),
        "maximum_embedding_error": float(
            np.max(np.abs(ordinary.embedding - disabled.embedding))
        ),
    }


def verify_library_matches_copied_code(
    x_input: np.ndarray,
    settings: PaCMAPSettings = PaCMAPSettings(),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Compare installed ``pacmap==0.9.1`` with the copied code mode."""
    try:
        installed_version = version("pacmap")
    except PackageNotFoundError:
        return {
            "status": "skipped",
            "reason": "the external pacmap package is not installed",
        }

    if installed_version != "0.9.1":
        return {
            "status": "skipped",
            "reason": f"pacmap {installed_version} is installed, not 0.9.1",
        }

    import pacmap as pacmap_library

    x_input = _as_features(x_input)
    copied = run_copied_original_pacmap(x_input, settings)
    library_model = pacmap_library.PaCMAP(
        n_components=settings.n_components,
        n_neighbors=settings.n_neighbors,
        MN_ratio=settings.mn_ratio,
        FP_ratio=settings.fp_ratio,
        distance="euclidean",
        lr=settings.learning_rate,
        num_iters=settings.num_iters,
        verbose=settings.verbose,
        apply_pca=True,
        random_state=settings.random_state,
        save_tree=False,
        knn_backend=settings.knn_backend,
    )
    library_embedding = library_model.fit_transform(
        x_input, init="pca", save_pairs=True
    )

    result = {
        "status": "pass",
        "library_version": installed_version,
        "neighbor_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            copied.pair_neighbors, library_model.pair_neighbors
        ),
        "mid_near_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            copied.pair_mn, library_model.pair_MN
        ),
        "further_pairs_equal": np.array_equal(
            copied.pair_fp, library_model.pair_FP
        ),
        "embedding_equal": np.array_equal(
            copied.embedding, library_embedding
        ),
        "maximum_embedding_error": float(
            np.max(np.abs(copied.embedding - library_embedding))
        ),
    }
    if not all(
        result[key]
        for key in (
            "neighbor_pairs_equal",
            "mid_near_pairs_equal",
            "further_pairs_equal",
            "embedding_equal",
        )
    ):
        result["status"] = "fail"
    return result


def make_synthetic_data(
    n_samples: int = 240,
    random_state: int = 42,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Small in-memory spatial dataset for a quick three-mode check."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(random_state)
    coordinates = rng.uniform(0.0, 1000.0, size=(n_samples, 3))
    x, y, z = coordinates.T
    latent = np.column_stack(
        [
            np.sin(x / 150.0) + np.cos(y / 180.0),
            (x - y) / 700.0 + np.sin(z / 120.0),
            np.cos((x + y) / 260.0) + z / 900.0,
        ]
    )
    loading = rng.normal(size=(3, 12))
    features = latent @ loading + rng.normal(
        0.0, 0.35, size=(n_samples, 12)
    )
    features = StandardScaler().fit_transform(features).astype(np.float32)
    return features, coordinates.astype(np.float32)


def main() -> None:
    """Run the small in-memory demonstration; no result files are written."""
    features, coordinates = make_synthetic_data()
    settings = PaCMAPSettings()

    for mode in (
        MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES,
        MODE_PACMAP_FEATURES_XYZ,
        MODE_SC_PACMAP,
    ):
        result, sc = run_mode(mode, features, coordinates, settings)
        print(mode, result.embedding.shape)
        if sc is not None:
            print(
                "  lambda=",
                round(sc.spatial_strength_lambda, 4),
                "k_spatial=",
                sc.k_spatial,
                "k_attribute=",
                sc.k_attribute,
            )

    print(
        "SC disabled check:",
        verify_sc_disabled_matches_original(
            features, coordinates, settings
        ),
    )
    print(
        "Library versus copied code:",
        verify_library_matches_copied_code(features, settings),
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: code/spasne/spasne_adapter.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

import numpy as np


def _resolve_spasne_dir(spasne_dir=None):
    if spasne_dir is None:
        spasne_dir = os.environ.get("SPASNE_DIR")
    if spasne_dir is None:
        spasne_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "spasne_original"
    return Path(spasne_dir).expanduser().resolve()


def _load_spasne_module(spasne_dir=None):
    spasne_dir = _resolve_spasne_dir(spasne_dir)
    module_path = spasne_dir / "spasne.py"
    executable = spasne_dir / "spasne"
    if not module_path.is_file() or not executable.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SpaSNE is not built at {spasne_dir}. Run 'make' in that directory "
            "on Linux or WSL, or set SPASNE_DIR."
        )

    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_sc_pacmap_spasne", module_path)
    if specification is None or specification.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unable to load SpaSNE wrapper from {module_path}")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _windows_to_wsl_path(path):
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["wsl.exe", "--exec", "wslpath", "-a", str(Path(path).resolve())],
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return result.stdout.strip()


def _run_spasne_wsl(features, coordinates, config, spasne_dir):
    spasne_dir = _resolve_spasne_dir(spasne_dir)
    bridge_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "spasne_wsl_bridge.py"
    if not bridge_path.is_file() or not (spasne_dir / "spasne").is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SpaSNE is not built at {spasne_dir}. Run 'make' there from WSL."
        )

    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sc_pacmap_spasne_") as temporary_directory:
            temporary_path = Path(temporary_directory)
            feature_path = temporary_path / "features.npy"
            coordinate_path = temporary_path / "coordinates.npy"
            config_path = temporary_path / "config.json"
            output_path = temporary_path / "embedding.npy"
            np.save(feature_path, features, allow_pickle=False)
            np.save(coordinate_path, coordinates, allow_pickle=False)
            config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")

            command = [
                "wsl.exe",
                "python3",
                _windows_to_wsl_path(bridge_path),
                _windows_to_wsl_path(spasne_dir),
                _windows_to_wsl_path(feature_path),
                _windows_to_wsl_path(coordinate_path),
                _windows_to_wsl_path(config_path),
                _windows_to_wsl_path(output_path),
            ]
            result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True)
            if result.returncode != 0:
                detail = result.stderr.strip() or result.stdout.strip()
                raise RuntimeError(f"SpaSNE failed in WSL: {detail}")
            if not output_path.is_file():
                raise RuntimeError("SpaSNE WSL bridge completed without an output file")
            return np.load(output_path, allow_pickle=False)
    except FileNotFoundError as error:
        raise RuntimeError("WSL is required to run SpaSNE from a Windows kernel") from error
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unable to translate a SpaSNE path for WSL: {error.stderr}") from error


def python_spasne(
    X_feat,
    coordinates,
    random_state=42,
    alpha=10.0,
    beta=5.0,
    exaggeration=4,
    perplexity=50,
    theta=0.5,
    max_iter=1000,
    use_pca=True,
    spasne_dir=None,
):
    """Run the pinned upstream SpaSNE implementation, using WSL on Windows."""
    features = np.asarray(X_feat, dtype=np.float64)
    spatial_coordinates = np.asarray(coordinates, dtype=np.float64)
    if features.ndim != 2 or spatial_coordinates.ndim != 2 or len(features) != len(spatial_coordinates):
        raise ValueError("X_feat and coordinates must be 2-D arrays with equal row counts")
    if len(features) < 2 or features.shape[1] < 1 or spatial_coordinates.shape[1] < 2:
        raise ValueError("SpaSNE requires at least two samples, one feature, and two coordinates")
    if not np.isfinite(features).all() or not np.isfinite(spatial_coordinates).all():
        raise ValueError("SpaSNE inputs must contain only finite values")
    if perplexity <= 0 or perplexity >= len(features):
        raise ValueError("perplexity must be positive and smaller than the sample count")

    config = {
        "alpha": alpha,
        "beta": beta,
        "exaggeration": exaggeration,
        "no_dims": 2,
        "perplexity": perplexity,
        "theta": theta,
        "randseed": int(random_state),
        "verbose": False,
        "initial_dims": min(features.shape[1], 200),
        "use_pca": use_pca,
        "max_iter": max_iter,
    }
    if os.name == "nt":
        embedding = _run_spasne_wsl(features, spatial_coordinates, config, spasne_dir)
    else:
        spasne = _load_spasne_module(spasne_dir)
        embedding = spasne.run_spasne(features, pixels=spatial_coordinates, **config)

    embedding = np.asarray(embedding, dtype=np.float64)
    if embedding.shape != (len(features), 2) or not np.isfinite(embedding).all():
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SpaSNE returned invalid embedding shape {embedding.shape}; "
            f"expected ({len(features)}, 2) with finite values"
        )
    return embedding

FILE: code/spasne/spasne_original/README.md

# 1. Introduction

As an extension of the t-distributed stochastic neighbor embedding (t-SNE), the 
spatially resolved t-SNE (SpaSNE) was designed to preserve both the global gene 
expression and the spatial structure for the spatially resolved profiling data. 
By leveraging both the gene expression and the spatial information, SpaSNE gives
a comprehensive low-dimensional visualization that could best reflect the 
molecular similarities of cells and the spatial interactions between cells.  

Paper: Dimensionality reduction for visualizing spatially resolved profiling 
        data using SpaSNE.

# 2. Result

Below is an example of the SpaSNE visualization for the mouse visual Cortex 
data.

![Fig](/Image/mouse_visualCortex_annotation_and_SpaSNE_result.png)

r1: Pearson correlation coefficient between pairwise Euclidean gene expression 
    distances and embedding distances of points, which was used to measure 
    gene expression preservation. 

r2: Pearson correlation coefficient between pairwise spatial position distances 
    and embedding distances of points, which was used to measure spatial 
    structure preservation.
    
s: The silhouette score, which is used to measure the goodness of clustering.
    
# 3. Environment setup and code compilation

__3.1. Download the package__

The package can be downloaded by running the following command in the terminal:
```
git clone https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE.git
```
Then, use
```
cd SpaSNE
```
to access the downloaded folder. 

If the "git clone" command does not work with your system, you can download the 
zip file from the website 
https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE.git and decompress it. Then, the folder 
that you need to access is SpaSNE-main. 

__3.2. Environment setup__

The package has been successuflly tested in a Linux environment of python 
version 3.8.8, pandas version 1.3.4, and g++ version 11.2.0. An option to set up 
the environment is to use Conda 
(https://docs.conda.io/projects/conda/en/latest/user-guide/install/index.html).

You can use the following command to create an environment for SpaSNE:
```
conda create -n myenv python=3.8.8 pandas=1.3.4
```

After the environment is created, you can use the following command to activate 
it:
```
conda activate myenv
```

Please install Jupyter Notebook from https://jupyter.org/install. For example, 
you can run
```
pip install notebook
```
in the terminal to install the classic Jupyter Notebook. 

The *.cpp files need g++ to compile. The code has been successfully tested under
g++ version 11.2.0. If your g++ version cannot successfully compile the code in 
the next step, please visit https://gcc.gnu.org/ to download the GCC 11.2. 

__3.3. Compilation__

After you set up the above environment, please run the following command in the 
terminal to generate the "spasne" executable file: 
```
make
```
Please see the Makefile file for other commands. The "make" in the system that 
the software was tested is default to use GNU Make 
(https://www.gnu.org/software/make/). If using "make" cannot generate the 
"spasne" executable file in your system, please use the following command in the
terminal to generate it: 
```
g++ sptree.cpp spasne.cpp spasne_main.cpp -o spasne -O2
```  

Now you could import spasne in the current directory. 

__3.4. Import spasne in different directories (optional)__

If you would like to import spasne in different directories, there is an option 
to make it work. Please run
```
python setup.py install --user &> log
```
in the terminal and then use
```
grep Installed log
```
to obtain the path that the SpaSNE software is installed. You will see something
like 
"Installed /home/chentang/.local/lib/python3.8/site-packages/spasne-1.0-py3.8.egg".

You need to copy the "spasne" executable file generated by running "gmake" in 
the terminal to the folder that the software is installed:
```
cp spasne /home/chentang/.local/lib/python3.8/site-packages/spasne-1.0-py3.8.egg/
```
Note, in the command above, please use your installed path to replace mine. 

After doing these successfully, you are supposed to be able to import spasne 
when you are using Python or Jupyter Notebook in other folders:
```
import spasne
```

The entire flow of "Environment setup and code compilation" took less than five
minutes on my local computer. 

__3.5. Build the Docker Image (optional)__

There is an option to build a Docker image using the provided Dockerfile. You 
can use the following command in the terminal to build the Docker image:
```
docker build -t spasne .
```

Once the image is built, you can use the following command:
```
docker run -it spasne bash
```
to run the container and start using SpaSNE.

# 4. Parameter setting

SpaSNE incorporates two pivotal parameters: the global gene expression weight 𝛼, which modulates the balance between local and global gene expression preservation, and the spatial weight 𝛽, which adjusts the balance between gene expressions and spatial structure. An increase in 𝛼 results in a larger rg and a smaller rs, whereas a higher 𝛽 enhances rs and diminishes rg. Therefore, maintaining an appropriate balance between 𝛼 and 𝛽 is crucial for achieving satisfactory preservation of both gene expressions and spatial configurations. We have devised a two-stage heuristic screening approach to identify the optimal parameters for specific datasets and have included a detailed tutorial using human breast cancer data in the [Tutorials](https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE/tree/main/Tutorials) folder.

In the latest version of SpaSNE, default settings are 𝛼 = 10 and 𝛽 = 5 when spatial information input is accessible, and 𝛼 = 5 and 𝛽 = 0 when it is absent. For details on additional parameters, please refer to the [spasne.py file](https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE/tree/main/spasne.py) file.

# 5. Examples

Please refer to the [Tutorials](https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE/tree/main/Tutorials) folder for detailed examples.

SpaSNE source and adaptation boundary
=====================================

Official repository
-------------------
https://github.com/Lin-Xu-lab/SpaSNE

Primary checkout commit
-----------------------
adef46ef7b4bc0a223b0cb6ccd39b9f3d02fc487

Restored upstream file
----------------------
spasne.cpp is copied byte-for-byte from upstream commit 1bb43e2. The primary
checkout commit deletes this file even though its unchanged Makefile still
requires it to compile SpaSNE.

License
