/* Please see the "LICENSE" file for the copyright information. */

/*
Notice: This SpaSNE software is adapted from the bhtsne code 
       (github.com/lvdmaaten/bhtsne). 
       Please see the "LICENSE" file for copyright details of the bhtsne 
       software. The implementation of the bhtsne software is described in the 
       publication "Accelerating t-SNE using Tree-Based Algorithms" 
       (https://jmlr.org/papers/v15/vandermaaten14a.html). 
*/

/* This code was adopted with minor modifications from Steve Hanov's great tutorial at http://stevehanov.ca/blog/index.php?id=130 */

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <queue>
#include <limits>
#include <cmath>

#ifndef VPTREE_H
#define VPTREE_H

class DataPoint
{
    int _ind;

public:
    double* _x;
    int _D;
    DataPoint() {
        _D = 1;
        _ind = -1;
        _x = NULL;
    }
    DataPoint(int D, int ind, double* x) {
        _D = D;
        _ind = ind;
        _x = (double*) malloc(_D * sizeof(double));
        for(int d = 0; d < _D; d++) _x[d] = x[d];
    }
    DataPoint(const DataPoint& other) {                     // this makes a deep copy -- should not free anything
        if(this != &other) {
            _D = other.dimensionality();
            _ind = other.index();
            _x = (double*) malloc(_D * sizeof(double));      
            for(int d = 0; d < _D; d++) _x[d] = other.x(d);
        }
    }
    ~DataPoint() { if(_x != NULL) free(_x); }
    DataPoint& operator= (const DataPoint& other) {         // asignment should free old object
        if(this != &other) {
            if(_x != NULL) free(_x);
            _D = other.dimensionality();
            _ind = other.index();
            _x = (double*) malloc(_D * sizeof(double));
            for(int d = 0; d < _D; d++) _x[d] = other.x(d);
        }
        return *this;
    }
    int index() const { return _ind; }
    int dimensionality() const { return _D; }
    double x(int d) const { return _x[d]; }
};

double euclidean_distance(const DataPoint &t1, const DataPoint &t2) {
    double dd = .0;
    double* x1 = t1._x;
    double* x2 = t2._x;
    double diff;
    for(int d = 0; d < t1._D; d++) {
        diff = (x1[d] - x2[d]);
        dd += diff * diff;
    }
    return sqrt(dd);
}


template<typename T, double (*distance)( const T&, const T& )>
class VpTree
{
public:
    
    // Default constructor
    VpTree() : _root(0) {}
    
    // Destructor
    ~VpTree() {
        delete _root;
    }

    // Function to create a new VpTree from data
    void create(const std::vector<T>& items) {
        delete _root;
        _items = items;
        _root = buildFromPoints(0, items.size());
    }
    
    // Function that uses the tree to find the k nearest neighbors of target
    void search(const T& target, int k, std::vector<T>* results, std::vector<double>* distances)
    {
        
        // Use a priority queue to store intermediate results on
        std::priority_queue<HeapItem> heap;
        
        // Variable that tracks the distance to the farthest point in our results
        _tau = DBL_MAX;
        
        // Perform the search
        search(_root, target, k, heap);
        
        // Gather final results
        results->clear(); distances->clear();
        while(!heap.empty()) {
            results->push_back(_items[heap.top().index]);
            distances->push_back(heap.top().dist);
            heap.pop();
        }
        
        // Results are in reverse order
        std::reverse(results->begin(), results->end());
        std::reverse(distances->begin(), distances->end());
    }
    
private:
    std::vector<T> _items;
    double _tau;
    
    // Single node of a VP tree (has a point and radius; left children are closer to point than the radius)
    struct Node
    {
        int index;              // index of point in node
        double threshold;       // radius(?)
        Node* left;             // points closer by than threshold
        Node* right;            // points farther away than threshold
        
        Node() :
        index(0), threshold(0.), left(0), right(0) {}
        
        ~Node() {               // destructor
            delete left;
            delete right;
        }
    }* _root;
    
    
    // An item on the intermediate result queue
    struct HeapItem {
        HeapItem( int index, double dist) :
        index(index), dist(dist) {}
        int index;
        double dist;
        bool operator<(const HeapItem& o) const {
            return dist < o.dist;
        }
    };
    
    // Distance comparator for use in std::nth_element
    struct DistanceComparator
    {
        const T& item;
        DistanceComparator(const T& item) : item(item) {}
        bool operator()(const T& a, const T& b) {
            return distance(item, a) < distance(item, b);
        }
    };
    
    // Function that (recursively) fills the tree
    Node* buildFromPoints( int lower, int upper )
    {
        if (upper == lower) {     // indicates that we're done here!
            return NULL;
        }
        
        // Lower index is center of current node
        Node* node = new Node();
        node->index = lower;
        
        if (upper - lower > 1) {      // if we did not arrive at leaf yet
            
            // Choose an arbitrary point and move it to the start
            int i = (int) ((double)rand() / RAND_MAX * (upper - lower - 1)) + lower;
            std::swap(_items[lower], _items[i]);
            
            // Partition around the median distance
            int median = (upper + lower) / 2;
            std::nth_element(_items.begin() + lower + 1,
                             _items.begin() + median,
                             _items.begin() + upper,
                             DistanceComparator(_items[lower]));
            
            // Threshold of the new node will be the distance to the median
            node->threshold = distance(_items[lower], _items[median]);
            
            // Recursively build tree
            node->index = lower;
            node->left = buildFromPoints(lower + 1, median);
            node->right = buildFromPoints(median, upper);
        }
        
        // Return result
        return node;
    }
    
    // Helper function that searches the tree    
    void search(Node* node, const T& target, int k, std::priority_queue<HeapItem>& heap)
    {
        if(node == NULL) return;     // indicates that we're done here
        
        // Compute distance between target and current node
        double dist = distance(_items[node->index], target);

        // If current node within radius tau
        if(dist < _tau) {
            if(heap.size() == k) heap.pop();                 // remove furthest node from result list (if we already have k results)
            heap.push(HeapItem(node->index, dist));           // add current node to result list
            if(heap.size() == k) _tau = heap.top().dist;     // update value of tau (farthest point in result list)
        }
        
        // Return if we arrived at a leaf
        if(node->left == NULL && node->right == NULL) {
            return;
        }
        
        // If the target lies within the radius of ball
        if(dist < node->threshold) {
            if(dist - _tau <= node->threshold) {         // if there can still be neighbors inside the ball, recursively search left child first
                search(node->left, target, k, heap);
            }
            
            if(dist + _tau >= node->threshold) {         // if there can still be neighbors outside the ball, recursively search right child
                search(node->right, target, k, heap);
            }
        
        // If the target lies outsize the radius of the ball
        } else {
            if(dist + _tau >= node->threshold) {         // if there can still be neighbors outside the ball, recursively search right child first
                search(node->right, target, k, heap);
            }
            
            if (dist - _tau <= node->threshold) {         // if there can still be neighbors inside the ball, recursively search left child
                search(node->left, target, k, heap);
            }
        }
    }
};
            
#endif

================================================================================
FILE: code/spasne/spasne_wsl_bridge.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import numpy as np


def load_spasne(spasne_dir):
    module_path = spasne_dir / 'spasne.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('_geopacmap_spasne_wsl', module_path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f'Unable to load SpaSNE wrapper from {module_path}')
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def main(args):
    if len(args) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            'Expected SpaSNE directory, feature, coordinate, config, and output paths'
        )

    spasne_dir, feature_path, coord_path, config_path, output_path = map(Path, args[1:])
    features = np.load(feature_path, allow_pickle=False)
    coords = np.load(coord_path, allow_pickle=False)
    config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

    spasne = load_spasne(spasne_dir)
    embedding = np.asarray(
        spasne.run_spasne(features, pixels=coords, **config),
        dtype=np.float64,
    )
    np.save(output_path, embedding, allow_pickle=False)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

# SC-PaCMAP data preparation

This notebook creates the two processed real datasets used by the paper benchmarks.

## Dataset 1: Critical Minerals in Ores (CMiO)

Geoscience Australia geochemistry is filtered to one UTM zone and prepared as a deposit-group benchmark with 35 chemical features.

## Dataset 2: North Batten drill-core geochemistry

The supplied 2015, 2018, and 2019 collar, survey, geology, assay, and lithology-code files are combined into a three-dimensional lithology benchmark with 46 shared `ME-MS61` measurements.

The synthetic benchmark generates its data inside its own notebook and requires no preparation input. Labels produced here are excluded from embedding and clustering and are used only for evaluation.

from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

def find_repository_root(start: Path) -> Path:
    ancestors = (start.resolve(), *start.resolve().parents)
    candidates = (*ancestors, *(path / "sc-pacmap-reproducibility" for path in ancestors))
    for candidate in candidates:
        if (candidate / "data_preparation").is_dir() and (candidate / "data").is_dir():
            return candidate
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not locate the sc-pacmap-reproducibility repository.")

--- Cell 5 [markdown] ---
# Part A - Critical Minerals in Ores

The cleaning is intentionally simple:

1. Keep one UTM zone (`54S`) so X and Y share one coordinate system.
2. Use the existing `DEPOSIT_GROUP` field as the discrete label.
3. Keep 35 well-populated chemical variables and retain complete rows across that panel.
4. Interpret each negative assay as a below-detection-limit (BDL) code, following the source geochemistry convention.
5. Replace each coded value $x < 0$ with $|x| / 2$. The encoded magnitude is used because companion detection-limit metadata are incomplete for some legacy records; this is left-censor substitution, not missing-value imputation.
6. Calculate `z` as negative interval midpoint depth.

--- Cell 6 [code] ---
CMIO_FEATURES = [
    "Y_PPM", "V_PPM", "LA_PPM", "U_PPM", "SR_PPM", "SN_PPM",
    "RB_PPM", "TH_PPM", "CS_PPM", "CE_PPM", "NB_PPM", "GA_PPM",
    "W_PPM", "HF_PPM", "TA_PPM", "ZR_PPM", "CR_PPM", "PR_PPM",
    "ND_PPM", "ER_PPM", "EU_PPM", "SM_PPM", "YB_PPM", "TB_PPM",
    "HO_PPM", "GD_PPM", "DY_PPM", "LU_PPM", "BA_PPM",
    "SIO2_WT_PERCENT", "TIO2_WT_PERCENT", "NA2O_WT_PERCENT",
    "K2O_WT_PERCENT", "AL2O3_WT_PERCENT", "TM_PPM",
]

cmio_raw = pd.read_csv(CMIO_FILE, low_memory=False)
print("Raw CMiO shape:", cmio_raw.shape)

--- Cell 7 [code] ---
# Keep one compatible projected coordinate system.
cmio = cmio_raw.loc[
    cmio_raw["SAMPLE_UTM_ZONE"].astype(str).str.strip().eq("54S")
].copy()

required_numeric = [
    "SAMPLE_EASTING", "SAMPLE_NORTHING", "TOP_DEPTH_M", "BASE_DEPTH_M",
    *CMIO_FEATURES,
]

# Convert bad text values to NaN, then keep complete rows.
for column in required_numeric:
    cmio[column] = pd.to_numeric(cmio[column], errors="coerce")

cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"] = cmio["DEPOSIT_GROUP"].astype("string").str.strip()
cmio = cmio.dropna(subset=["LABEL_deposit_group", *required_numeric]).copy()
cmio = cmio.loc[cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"].ne("")].copy()

# GA negative assay codes denote below-detection-limit (BDL) observations.
# Substitute half the encoded limit (-v -> v / 2); this handles left censoring,
# not missing data, and avoids incomplete legacy companion detection-limit fields.
cmio[CMIO_FEATURES] = cmio[CMIO_FEATURES].mask(
    cmio[CMIO_FEATURES] < 0,
    cmio[CMIO_FEATURES].abs() / 2,
)

# Keep labels that have enough examples for evaluation.
class_counts = cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"].value_counts()
keep_labels = class_counts[class_counts >= MIN_CMIO_CLASS_SIZE].index
cmio = cmio.loc[cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"].isin(keep_labels)].copy()

# Build common spatial and identifier columns.
cmio["sample_id"] = cmio["SAMPLE_UID"].astype(str)
cmio["group_id"] = cmio["DEPOSIT_NAME"].astype(str)
cmio["x"] = cmio["SAMPLE_EASTING"]
cmio["y"] = cmio["SAMPLE_NORTHING"]
cmio["z"] = -(cmio["TOP_DEPTH_M"] + cmio["BASE_DEPTH_M"]) / 2

label_order = sorted(cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"].unique())
label_map = {label: number for number, label in enumerate(label_order)}
cmio["label_id"] = cmio["LABEL_deposit_group"].map(label_map).astype(int)

cmio_ready = cmio[
    [
        "sample_id", "group_id", "x", "y", "z",

    ]
].reset_index(drop=True)

print("Number of classes:", cmio_ready["LABEL_deposit_group"].nunique())

--- Cell 9 [code] ---
def add_interval_label(samples, intervals, label_column):
    """Assign a label when one geology interval covers at least half the sample."""
    output = []

    for drill_id, sample_group in samples.groupby("DRILL_ID", sort=False):
        interval_group = intervals.loc[intervals["DRILL_ID"].eq(drill_id)].copy()
        interval_group = interval_group.dropna(subset=["FROM", "TO", label_column])
        if interval_group.empty:
            continue

        overlap = np.maximum(
            0,
            np.minimum(sample_group["to_m"].to_numpy()[:, None], interval_group["TO"].to_numpy())
            - np.maximum(sample_group["from_m"].to_numpy()[:, None], interval_group["FROM"].to_numpy()),
        )
        positions = overlap.argmax(axis=1)
        sample_lengths = sample_group["to_m"].to_numpy() - sample_group["from_m"].to_numpy()
        valid = (sample_lengths > 0) & (overlap.max(axis=1) >= 0.5 * sample_lengths)

        matched = sample_group.iloc[np.flatnonzero(valid)].copy()
        matched["label_lithology"] = interval_group.iloc[positions[valid]][label_column].to_numpy()
        output.append(matched)

    if not output:
        return samples.iloc[0:0].assign(label_lithology=pd.Series(dtype="string"))
    return pd.concat(output, ignore_index=True)

--- Cell 10 [markdown] ---
## Shared interval and desurvey helpers

The preparation uses interval-overlap labels and minimum-curvature desurveying. When the first survey station is below zero depth, its measured direction is extended back to the collar. Samples beyond the final station are flagged and excluded.

--- Cell 11 [code] ---
def prepare_survey(hole_surveys):
    """Return clean measured-depth, dip, and grid-azimuth survey stations."""
    data = hole_surveys[["Depth", "Dip", "Azimuth"]].copy()
    data.columns = ["DEPTH", "DIP", "azimuth_deg"]
    data = data.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce").dropna()
    data = data.loc[
        data["DEPTH"].ge(0)
        & data["DIP"].between(-90, 90)
        & data["azimuth_deg"].between(0, 360, inclusive="left")
    ]
    data = data.sort_values("DEPTH").drop_duplicates("DEPTH", keep="last")

    if data.empty:
        return data
    if data.iloc[0]["DEPTH"] > 0:
        first_station = data.iloc[[0]].copy()
        first_station["DEPTH"] = 0.0
        data = pd.concat([first_station, data], ignore_index=True)

    return data


def minimum_curvature_xyz(sample_depths, survey_rows, collar_x, collar_y, collar_z):
    """Calculate XYZ at requested measured depths using minimum curvature."""
    sample_depths = np.asarray(sample_depths, dtype=float)
    station_depths = survey_rows["DEPTH"].to_numpy(float)
    station_dips = survey_rows["DIP"].to_numpy(float)
    station_azimuths = survey_rows["azimuth_deg"].to_numpy(float)

    # Source dip is negative down; inclination is measured from vertical down.
    station_inclinations = np.radians(90.0 + station_dips)
    station_azimuths = np.unwrap(np.radians(station_azimuths))

    nodes = np.unique(np.round(np.concatenate(([0.0], station_depths, sample_depths)), 6))
    inclinations = np.interp(nodes, station_depths, station_inclinations)
    azimuths = np.interp(nodes, station_depths, station_azimuths)

    east = np.zeros(len(nodes))
    north = np.zeros(len(nodes))
    down = np.zeros(len(nodes))

    for index in range(1, len(nodes)):
        length = nodes[index] - nodes[index - 1]
        inc_1, inc_2 = inclinations[index - 1], inclinations[index]
        azi_1, azi_2 = azimuths[index - 1], azimuths[index]

        cosine_dogleg = (
            np.cos(inc_1) * np.cos(inc_2)
            + np.sin(inc_1) * np.sin(inc_2) * np.cos(azi_2 - azi_1)
        )
        dogleg = np.arccos(np.clip(cosine_dogleg, -1.0, 1.0))
        ratio_factor = 1.0 if dogleg < 1e-12 else 2.0 * np.tan(dogleg / 2.0) / dogleg

        north[index] = north[index - 1] + (
            length / 2.0
            * (np.sin(inc_1) * np.cos(azi_1) + np.sin(inc_2) * np.cos(azi_2))
            * ratio_factor
        )
        east[index] = east[index - 1] + (
            length / 2.0
            * (np.sin(inc_1) * np.sin(azi_1) + np.sin(inc_2) * np.sin(azi_2))
            * ratio_factor
        )
        down[index] = down[index - 1] + (
            length / 2.0
            * (np.cos(inc_1) + np.cos(inc_2))
            * ratio_factor
        )

    sample_positions = np.searchsorted(nodes, np.round(sample_depths, 6))
    x = collar_x + east[sample_positions]
    y = collar_y + north[sample_positions]
    z = collar_z - down[sample_positions]
    return x, y, z

--- Cell 12 [markdown] ---
# Part C - North Batten drill-core geochemistry

This section combines the supplied 2015, 2018, and 2019 campaigns with transparent rules:

1. Read the Northern Territory tabular templates from their `H1000` data headers.
2. Keep direct `ME-MS61` measurements shared by all three campaigns.
3. Keep features present in at least 80% of chemistry-bearing rows in every campaign, then use complete rows only.
4. Replace a reported `<v` result with `v / 2` and a reported `>v` result with `v`.
5. Assign the primary lithology when one geology interval covers at least half of the assay interval.
6. Convert lithology codes to readable labels and keep final classes with at least 20 intervals.
7. Calculate MGA94 Zone 53 `x` and `y` by minimum curvature. `z` is vertical displacement below the collar because later collar elevations are missing or zero placeholders.
8. Exclude samples beyond the final survey station. No assay imputation or survey extrapolation is used.

--- Cell 13 [code] ---
def read_nt_template(path):
    """Read one NT exploration template without losing duplicate headers."""
    header_rows = {}
    data_rows = []

    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig") as source:
        for line_number, line in enumerate(source, start=1):
            parts = line.rstrip("\r\n").split("\t")
            row_type = parts[0].strip()
            if row_type in {"H1000", "H1001", "H1002", "H1003"}:
                header_rows[row_type] = parts[1:]
            elif row_type == "D":
                data_rows.append((line_number, parts[1:]))

    if "H1000" not in header_rows:
        raise ValueError(f"No H1000 data header found in {path.name}")

    source_names = header_rows["H1000"]
    width = len(source_names)
    column_keys = [f"column_{position:03d}" for position in range(width)]

    clean_rows = []
    for line_number, values in data_rows:
        if len(values) > width and any(value.strip() for value in values[width:]):
            raise ValueError(f"Too many fields in {path.name} at line {line_number}")
        clean_rows.append((values + [""] * width)[:width])

    data = pd.DataFrame(clean_rows, columns=column_keys)
    schema = pd.DataFrame({
        "column_key": column_keys,
        "source_name": source_names,
        "unit": (header_rows.get("H1001", []) + [""] * width)[:width],
        "method": (header_rows.get("H1002", []) + [""] * width)[:width],
        "detection_limit": (header_rows.get("H1003", []) + [""] * width)[:width],
    })
    return data, schema


def simple_name(value):
    """Make source header spelling differences easy to compare."""
    return "".join(character.lower() for character in str(value) if character.isalnum())


def nt_column_key(schema, aliases):
    wanted = {simple_name(alias) for alias in aliases}
    matches = schema.loc[
        schema["source_name"].map(simple_name).isin(wanted), "column_key"
    ].tolist()
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Expected one of {aliases}, found {len(matches)} matches")
    return matches[0]


def concentration_unit(value):
    unit = str(value).strip().lower()
    if unit in {"ppm", "partspermillion"}:
        return "PPM"
    if unit in {"%", "percent", "pct"}:
        return "PCT"
    return None


def assay_number(value, detection_limit):
    """Convert source assay text while preserving documented censoring."""
    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text or text.upper() in {"NULL", "NA", "N/A", "NAN", "-"}:
        return np.nan

    multiplier = 1.0
    if text.startswith("<"):
        multiplier = 0.5
        text = text[1:].strip()
    elif text.startswith(">"):
        text = text[1:].strip()

    try:
        number = float(text) * multiplier
    except ValueError:
        return np.nan

    limit = pd.to_numeric(detection_limit, errors="coerce")
    if number < 0 and pd.notna(limit) and np.isclose(abs(number), limit):
        return abs(number) / 2.0
    return number


def prepare_north_batten_assays(year, path):
    """Return valid assay intervals and comparable ME-MS61 features."""
    raw, schema = read_nt_template(path)
    hole_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Hole_id"])
    sample_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Sample_id", "Sample ID"])
    from_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["From", "Depth From"])
    to_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["To", "Depth To"])

    output = pd.DataFrame({
        "source_year": year,
        "source_order": np.arange(len(raw)),
        "DRILL_ID": raw[hole_key].astype("string").str.strip(),
        "source_sample_id": raw[sample_key].astype("string").str.strip(),
        "from_m": pd.to_numeric(raw[from_key], errors="coerce"),
        "to_m": pd.to_numeric(raw[to_key], errors="coerce"),
    })

    feature_sources = []
    for row in schema.itertuples(index=False):
        unit = concentration_unit(row.unit)
        if str(row.method).strip().upper() != "ME-MS61" or unit is None:
            continue
        analyte = simple_name(row.source_name).upper()
        if not analyte:
            continue
        feature_sources.append(
            (row.column_key, f"{analyte}_ME_MS61_{unit}", row.detection_limit)
        )

    feature_names = [feature for _, feature, _ in feature_sources]
    if len(feature_names) != len(set(feature_names)):
        raise ValueError(f"Duplicate normalized ME-MS61 features in {path.name}")

    for column_key, feature, detection_limit in feature_sources:
        output[feature] = raw[column_key].map(
            lambda value, limit=detection_limit: assay_number(value, limit)
        )

    valid_interval = (
        output["DRILL_ID"].notna()
        & output["DRILL_ID"].ne("")
        & output["from_m"].notna()
        & output["to_m"].notna()
        & output["from_m"].lt(output["to_m"])
    )
    output = output.loc[valid_interval].copy()
    output = output.loc[output[feature_names].notna().any(axis=1)].copy()

    print(
        f"{year}: {len(raw)} source assay rows, {len(output)} valid rows "
        f"with ME-MS61 chemistry, {len(feature_names)} candidate features"
    )
    return output, feature_names

north_batten_assays_by_year = {}
north_batten_candidates_by_year = {}

for year, files in NORTH_BATTEN_FILES.items():
    assay_path = NORTH_BATTEN_RAW_DIR / str(year) / files["assays"]
    assays, candidate_features = prepare_north_batten_assays(year, assay_path)
    north_batten_assays_by_year[year] = assays
    north_batten_candidates_by_year[year] = set(candidate_features)

shared_features = sorted(set.intersection(*north_batten_candidates_by_year.values()))
if not shared_features:
    raise ValueError("No method-and-unit-qualified ME-MS61 features are shared by all campaigns")

coverage_by_year = pd.DataFrame({
    year: assays[shared_features].notna().mean()
    for year, assays in north_batten_assays_by_year.items()
})
NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES = coverage_by_year.index[
    coverage_by_year.ge(MIN_NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURE_COVERAGE).all(axis=1)
].tolist()

if len(NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES) < 2:
    raise ValueError("The North Batten coverage rule retained fewer than two features")

clean_assay_parts = []
for year, assays in north_batten_assays_by_year.items():
    assays = assays.copy()
    assays["observed_features"] = assays[NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES].notna().sum(axis=1)
    interval_keys = ["source_year", "DRILL_ID", "from_m", "to_m"]
    duplicate_rows = int(assays.duplicated(interval_keys, keep=False).sum())
    assays = (
        assays.sort_values([*interval_keys, "observed_features", "source_order"])
        .drop_duplicates(interval_keys, keep="last")
        .drop(columns="observed_features")

    before_complete_case = len(assays)
    assays = assays.dropna(subset=NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES).copy()
    print(
        f"{year}: {duplicate_rows} duplicate interval rows considered; "
        f"{len(assays)} of {before_complete_case} unique rows are complete"
    )
    clean_assay_parts.append(assays)

north_batten = pd.concat(clean_assay_parts, ignore_index=True)
if set(north_batten["source_year"].unique()) != set(NORTH_BATTEN_FILES):
    raise ValueError("Complete-case filtering removed an entire North Batten campaign")
if (north_batten[NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES] < 0).any().any():
    raise ValueError("Negative values found in retained North Batten chemistry")

north_batten["depth_m"] = (north_batten["from_m"] + north_batten["to_m"]) / 2
fallback_id = (
    north_batten["DRILL_ID"].astype(str)
    + "_"
    + north_batten["from_m"].map(lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
    + "_"
    + north_batten["to_m"].map(lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
)
source_id = north_batten["source_sample_id"].fillna("").str.strip()
source_id = source_id.mask(source_id.eq(""), fallback_id)
north_batten["sample_id"] = north_batten["source_year"].astype(str) + "_" + source_id
north_batten["group_id"] = north_batten["DRILL_ID"].astype(str)

if north_batten["sample_id"].duplicated().any():
    raise ValueError("Duplicate North Batten sample identifiers remain")

print("\nComplete comparable assay rows:", len(north_batten))
print(north_batten["source_year"].value_counts().sort_index())

--- Cell 15 [code] ---
def prepare_north_batten_geology(year, path):
    """Return valid primary-lithology intervals for one campaign."""
    raw, schema = read_nt_template(path)
    hole_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Hole_id"])
    from_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["DEPTH_FROM", "Depth From"])
    to_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["DEPTH_TO", "Depth To"])
    lithology_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Lithology"])

    geology = pd.DataFrame({
        "source_year": year,
        "DRILL_ID": raw[hole_key].astype("string").str.strip(),
        "FROM": pd.to_numeric(raw[from_key], errors="coerce"),
        "TO": pd.to_numeric(raw[to_key], errors="coerce"),
        "lithology_code": raw[lithology_key].astype("string").str.strip().str.upper(),
    })
    valid = (
        geology["DRILL_ID"].notna()
        & geology["FROM"].notna()
        & geology["TO"].notna()
        & geology["FROM"].lt(geology["TO"])
        & geology["lithology_code"].notna()
        & geology["lithology_code"].ne("")
    )
    return geology.loc[valid].copy()


def add_north_batten_lithology(samples, geology):
    """Assign the largest geology overlap when it covers half the assay."""
    output = []
    group_columns = ["source_year", "DRILL_ID"]

    for group_key, sample_group in samples.groupby(group_columns, sort=False):
        year, drill_id = group_key
        interval_group = geology.loc[
            geology["source_year"].eq(year) & geology["DRILL_ID"].eq(drill_id)
        ]
        if interval_group.empty:
            continue

        overlap = np.maximum(
            0,
            np.minimum(sample_group["to_m"].to_numpy()[:, None], interval_group["TO"].to_numpy())
            - np.maximum(sample_group["from_m"].to_numpy()[:, None], interval_group["FROM"].to_numpy()),
        )
        positions = overlap.argmax(axis=1)
        sample_lengths = sample_group["to_m"].to_numpy() - sample_group["from_m"].to_numpy()
        valid = overlap.max(axis=1) >= 0.5 * sample_lengths

        matched = sample_group.iloc[np.flatnonzero(valid)].copy()
        matched["lithology_code"] = interval_group.iloc[positions[valid]][
            "lithology_code"
        ].to_numpy()
        output.append(matched)

    if not output:
        return samples.iloc[0:0].assign(lithology_code=pd.Series(dtype="string"))
    return pd.concat(output, ignore_index=True)


geology_parts = []
code_parts = []
for year, files in NORTH_BATTEN_FILES.items():
    year_folder = NORTH_BATTEN_RAW_DIR / str(year)
    geology_parts.append(
        prepare_north_batten_geology(year, year_folder / files["geology"])
    )

    separator = "\t" if year_folder.joinpath(files["codes"]).suffix.lower() == ".txt" else ","
    codes = pd.read_csv(year_folder / files["codes"], sep=separator, dtype="string")
    codes.columns = [column.strip() for column in codes.columns]
    codes = codes.loc[
        codes["FieldName"].str.strip().str.upper().eq("LITHOLOGY"),
        ["Code", "Description"],
    ].copy()
    codes["source_year"] = year
    codes["lithology_code"] = codes["Code"].str.strip().str.upper()
    codes["description"] = codes["Description"].str.strip()
    code_parts.append(codes[["source_year", "lithology_code", "description"]])

north_batten_geology = pd.concat(geology_parts, ignore_index=True)
all_lithology_codes = pd.concat(code_parts, ignore_index=True)
description_counts = all_lithology_codes.groupby("lithology_code")["description"].nunique()
conflicting_codes = description_counts[description_counts > 1].index.tolist()
if conflicting_codes:
    print("Lithology descriptions updated between campaigns:", conflicting_codes)

# Prefer the most recent supplied description when a codebook was updated.
lithology_lookup = (
    all_lithology_codes.sort_values("source_year")
    .drop_duplicates("lithology_code", keep="last")
    .set_index("lithology_code")["description"]
)

rows_before_geology = len(north_batten)
north_batten = add_north_batten_lithology(north_batten, north_batten_geology)
non_rock_codes = {"CLS", "CVT", "NCP", "NL", "NR"}
non_rock_rows = int(north_batten["lithology_code"].isin(non_rock_codes).sum())
north_batten["lithology_description"] = north_batten["lithology_code"].map(lithology_lookup)
unmapped_rows = int(north_batten["lithology_description"].isna().sum())
north_batten = north_batten.loc[
    ~north_batten["lithology_code"].isin(non_rock_codes)
    & north_batten["lithology_description"].notna()
].copy()
north_batten["label_lithology"] = (
    north_batten["lithology_code"] + " - " + north_batten["lithology_description"]
)

print("Assay rows before geology matching:", rows_before_geology)
print("Rows with at least 50% primary-lithology coverage:", len(north_batten) + non_rock_rows + unmapped_rows)
print("Excluded non-rock rows:", non_rock_rows)
print("Excluded unmapped lithology rows:", unmapped_rows)
print("Rows with readable rock labels:", len(north_batten))
print(north_batten["label_lithology"].value_counts())

--- Cell 16 [code] ---
def optional_nt_column_key(schema, aliases):
    wanted = {simple_name(alias) for alias in aliases}
    matches = schema.loc[
        schema["source_name"].map(simple_name).isin(wanted), "column_key"
    ].tolist()
    return matches[0] if len(matches) == 1 else None


def nt_metadata_value(path, record_name):
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig") as source:
        for line in source:
            parts = line.rstrip("\r\n").split("\t")
            if simple_name(parts[0]) == simple_name(record_name):
                return parts[2].strip() if len(parts) > 2 else ""
    return ""


def prepare_north_batten_collars(year, path):
    """Return unique MGA94 Zone 53 collar XY positions."""
    datum = nt_metadata_value(path, "H0501").upper()
    zone = nt_metadata_value(path, "H0531")
    if datum != "GDA94" or zone != "53":
        raise ValueError(f"Unexpected coordinate system in {path.name}: {datum}, zone {zone}")

    raw, schema = read_nt_template(path)
    hole_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Hole_id"])
    x_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["MGA_E", "Easting_MGA"])
    y_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["MGA_N", "Northing_MGA"])
    collars = pd.DataFrame({
        "source_year": year,
        "DRILL_ID": raw[hole_key].astype("string").str.strip(),
        "collar_x": pd.to_numeric(raw[x_key], errors="coerce"),
        "collar_y": pd.to_numeric(raw[y_key], errors="coerce"),
    }).dropna(subset=["DRILL_ID", "collar_x", "collar_y"])

    if collars.duplicated(["source_year", "DRILL_ID"]).any():
        raise ValueError(f"Duplicate valid collars in {path.name}")
    return collars


def prepare_north_batten_surveys(year, path):
    """Return measured survey stations and their source azimuth columns."""
    raw, schema = read_nt_template(path)
    hole_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Hole_id"])
    depth_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Surveyed_Depth", "Surveyed Depth"])
    dip_key = nt_column_key(schema, ["Dip"])
    grid_key = optional_nt_column_key(schema, ["Azimuth_grid"])
    magnetic_key = optional_nt_column_key(schema, ["Azimith_mag", "Azimuth_MAG"])

    surveys = pd.DataFrame({
        "source_year": year,
        "DRILL_ID": raw[hole_key].astype("string").str.strip(),
        "Depth": pd.to_numeric(raw[depth_key], errors="coerce"),
        "Dip": pd.to_numeric(raw[dip_key], errors="coerce"),
        "azimuth_grid_source": (
            pd.to_numeric(raw[grid_key], errors="coerce") if grid_key else np.nan
        ),
        "azimuth_magnetic": (
            pd.to_numeric(raw[magnetic_key], errors="coerce") if magnetic_key else np.nan
        ),
    })
    return surveys


collar_parts = []
survey_parts = []
for year, files in NORTH_BATTEN_FILES.items():
    year_folder = NORTH_BATTEN_RAW_DIR / str(year)
    collar_parts.append(
        prepare_north_batten_collars(year, year_folder / files["collars"])
    )
    survey_parts.append(
        prepare_north_batten_surveys(year, year_folder / files["surveys"])
    )

north_batten_collars = pd.concat(collar_parts, ignore_index=True)
north_batten_surveys = pd.concat(survey_parts, ignore_index=True)

reference_rows = north_batten_surveys.loc[
    north_batten_surveys["source_year"].eq(2015)
    & north_batten_surveys["azimuth_grid_source"].notna()
    & north_batten_surveys["azimuth_magnetic"].notna()
    & (
        north_batten_surveys["azimuth_grid_source"].ne(0)
        | north_batten_surveys["azimuth_magnetic"].ne(0)
    )
].copy()
reference_rows["grid_minus_magnetic"] = (
    reference_rows["azimuth_grid_source"]
    - reference_rows["azimuth_magnetic"]
    + 180.0
) % 360.0 - 180.0
magnetic_to_grid = float(reference_rows["grid_minus_magnetic"].median())
correction_spread = float(
    (reference_rows["grid_minus_magnetic"] - magnetic_to_grid).abs().max()
)
if len(reference_rows) < 10 or correction_spread > 1.0:
    raise ValueError("The 2015 magnetic-to-grid correction is not sufficiently stable")

north_batten_surveys["Azimuth"] = north_batten_surveys["azimuth_grid_source"]
north_batten_surveys["azimuth_reference"] = "SOURCE_GRID"
later_campaign = north_batten_surveys["source_year"].ne(2015)
north_batten_surveys.loc[later_campaign, "Azimuth"] = (
    north_batten_surveys.loc[later_campaign, "azimuth_magnetic"] + magnetic_to_grid
) % 360.0
north_batten_surveys.loc[later_campaign, "azimuth_reference"] = (
    "MAGNETIC_PLUS_2015_OFFSET"
)

print(
    "Magnetic-to-grid correction:",
    f"{magnetic_to_grid:.2f} degrees from {len(reference_rows)} paired stations;",
    f"maximum deviation {correction_spread:.2f} degrees",
)

collar_lookup = north_batten_collars.set_index(["source_year", "DRILL_ID"])
coordinate_parts = []
missing_collar_rows = 0
missing_survey_rows = 0
beyond_survey_rows = 0

for group_key, samples in north_batten.groupby(["source_year", "DRILL_ID"], sort=False):
    year, drill_id = group_key
    if group_key not in collar_lookup.index:
        missing_collar_rows += len(samples)
        continue

    hole_surveys = north_batten_surveys.loc[
        north_batten_surveys["source_year"].eq(year)
        & north_batten_surveys["DRILL_ID"].eq(drill_id)
    ]
    survey_rows = prepare_survey(hole_surveys)
    if survey_rows.empty:
        missing_survey_rows += len(samples)
        continue

    within_survey = samples["depth_m"].le(survey_rows["DEPTH"].max())
    beyond_survey_rows += int((~within_survey).sum())
    samples = samples.loc[within_survey].copy()
    if samples.empty:
        continue

    collar = collar_lookup.loc[group_key]
    samples["x"], samples["y"], samples["z"] = minimum_curvature_xyz(
        samples["depth_m"].to_numpy(),
        survey_rows,
        float(collar["collar_x"]),
        float(collar["collar_y"]),
        0.0,
    )
    samples["azimuth_reference"] = hole_surveys["azimuth_reference"].iloc[0]
    samples["survey_extrapolated"] = False
    coordinate_parts.append(samples)

if not coordinate_parts:
    raise ValueError("No North Batten intervals have valid collar and survey data")
north_batten = pd.concat(coordinate_parts, ignore_index=True)

final_counts = north_batten["label_lithology"].value_counts()
final_labels = final_counts[final_counts >= MIN_NORTH_BATTEN_CLASS_SIZE].index
north_batten_ready = north_batten.loc[
    north_batten["label_lithology"].isin(final_labels)
].copy()

label_order = sorted(north_batten_ready["label_lithology"].unique())
label_map = {label: number for number, label in enumerate(label_order)}
north_batten_ready["label_id"] = (
    north_batten_ready["label_lithology"].map(label_map).astype(int)
)

output_columns = [
    "sample_id", "group_id", "source_year", "depth_m", "x", "y", "z",
    "lithology_code", "label_lithology", "label_id", "azimuth_reference",
    "survey_extrapolated", *NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES,
]
north_batten_ready = north_batten_ready[output_columns].reset_index(drop=True)

if set(north_batten_ready["source_year"].unique()) != set(NORTH_BATTEN_FILES):
    raise ValueError("Coordinate or class filtering removed an entire North Batten campaign")
if (north_batten_ready["z"] > 1e-9).any():
    raise ValueError("Collar-relative North Batten z must be nonpositive")

print("Rows excluded for missing collars:", missing_collar_rows)
print("Rows excluded for missing surveys:", missing_survey_rows)
print("Rows excluded beyond final survey stations:", beyond_survey_rows)
print("Final North Batten shape:", north_batten_ready.shape)
print("Final assay features:", len(NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES))
print("Final lithology classes:", north_batten_ready["label_lithology"].nunique())
print("Final drillholes:", north_batten_ready["group_id"].nunique())
print("Rows by source year:")
print(north_batten_ready["source_year"].value_counts().sort_index())
print("Final class counts:")
print(north_batten_ready["label_lithology"].value_counts())

--- Cell 17 [code] ---
north_batten_output = OUTPUT_DIR / "north_batten_sc_pacmap_ready.csv"
north_batten_ready.to_csv(north_batten_output, index=False)

print("Saved:", north_batten_output)

--- Cell 18 [markdown] ---
# Important paper notes

- **CMiO:** `x` and `y` are source UTM Zone 54S coordinates. `z` is negative interval midpoint depth, not absolute elevation.
- **North Batten:** `x` and `y` are MGA94 Zone 53 coordinates. `z` is minimum-curvature vertical displacement relative to the collar (`z = 0`), not AHD elevation.
- North Batten combines the supplied 2015, 2018, and 2019 campaigns. Later magnetic surveys are converted to grid bearings with the median offset measured from paired 2015 source bearings.
- North Batten features are shared direct `ME-MS61` measurements with at least 80% coverage in every campaign. Censored results use half the detection limit, and rows are complete across all retained features.
- Feature values remain unscaled. Standardisation is performed in each benchmark.

--- Cell 19 [markdown] ---
# Final checks

These checks verify that both outputs are immediately usable for ML: no missing or non-finite values, unique sample identifiers, no constant features, multiple classes, all three North Batten campaigns represented, no negative retained North Batten concentrations, and no survey extrapolation.

--- Cell 20 [code] ---
def validate_dataset(name, data, features, label_column):
    required = [
        "sample_id", "group_id", "x", "y", "z",
        label_column, "label_id", *features,
    ]
    numeric = data[["x", "y", "z", *features]].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    result = {
        "dataset": name,
        "rows": len(data),
        "features": len(features),
        "classes": data[label_column].nunique(),
        "groups": data["group_id"].nunique(),
        "missing_required_values": int(data[required].isna().sum().sum()),
        "nonfinite_numeric_values": int((~np.isfinite(numeric)).sum()),
        "duplicate_sample_ids": int(data["sample_id"].duplicated().sum()),
        "constant_features": int(data[features].nunique(dropna=False).le(1).sum()),
    }

    assert result["rows"] > 0
    assert result["features"] > 0
    assert result["classes"] > 1
    assert result["missing_required_values"] == 0
    assert result["nonfinite_numeric_values"] == 0
    assert result["duplicate_sample_ids"] == 0
    assert result["constant_features"] == 0
    return result


summary = pd.DataFrame([
    validate_dataset(
        "CMiO geochemistry", cmio_ready, CMIO_FEATURES, "LABEL_deposit_group"
    ),
    validate_dataset(
        "North Batten assays",
        north_batten_ready,
        NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES,
        "label_lithology",
    ),
])

assert north_batten_ready["label_lithology"].value_counts().min() >= MIN_NORTH_BATTEN_CLASS_SIZE
assert set(north_batten_ready["source_year"].unique()) == set(NORTH_BATTEN_FILES)
assert not north_batten_ready["survey_extrapolated"].any()
assert not (north_batten_ready[NORTH_BATTEN_FEATURES] < 0).any().any()
assert north_batten_ready["x"].between(100_000, 900_000).all()
assert north_batten_ready["y"].between(0, 10_000_000).all()
assert north_batten_ready["z"].le(1e-9).all()

straight_survey = pd.DataFrame({
    "DEPTH": [0.0, 100.0],
    "DIP": [-90.0, -90.0],
    "azimuth_deg": [0.0, 0.0],
})
straight_x, straight_y, straight_z = minimum_curvature_xyz(
    np.array([100.0]), straight_survey, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
)
assert np.allclose([straight_x[0], straight_y[0], straight_z[0]], [0.0, 0.0, -100.0])

summary

--- Cell 1 [markdown] ---
# CMiO SC-PaCMAP benchmark

## Study overview

**Research question.** Does SC-PaCMAP produce K-means partitions with a better balance of spatial contiguity and multivariate geochemical delineation than coordinate-aware UMAP, PaCMAP, LocalMAP, and SpaSNE?

This deterministic, full-population benchmark evaluates seven unsupervised embeddings. Deposit-group labels do not enter numeric preprocessing, embedding fitting, or K-means fitting. They define the evaluation classes and the label-informed $K=7$ reference; ARI measures evaluation-only agreement on the same full population.

The notebook first isolates the PaCMAP design choices in a focused six-method comparison, then evaluates all seven candidates using the same clustering and spatial-evaluation settings.

--- Cell 2 [code] ---
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

def find_repository_root(start: Path) -> Path:
    ancestors = (start.resolve(), *start.resolve().parents)
    candidates = (*ancestors, *(path / "sc-pacmap-reproducibility" for path in ancestors))
    for candidate in candidates:
        if (
            (candidate / "code" / "sc_pacmap" / "sc_pacmap.py").is_file()
            and (candidate / "data" / "processed").is_dir()
        ):
            return candidate
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not locate the sc-pacmap-reproducibility repository.")


ROOT = find_repository_root(Path.cwd())
SC_MODULE_DIR = ROOT / "code" / "sc_pacmap"
SPASNE_MODULE_DIR = ROOT / "code" / "spasne"
for module_dir in (SC_MODULE_DIR, SPASNE_MODULE_DIR):
    if str(module_dir) not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(module_dir))

from spasne_adapter import python_spasne

sc_pacmap_module = importlib.import_module("sc_pacmap")
sc_pacmap_module = importlib.reload(sc_pacmap_module)
PaCMAPSettings = sc_pacmap_module.PaCMAPSettings
run_legacy_sc_pacmap = sc_pacmap_module.run_legacy_sc_pacmap
run_sc_pacmap = sc_pacmap_module.run_sc_pacmap
verify_library_matches_copied_code = sc_pacmap_module.verify_library_matches_copied_code

if version("pacmap") != "0.9.1":
    raise RuntimeError("This benchmark requires pacmap==0.9.1.")

    raise RuntimeError("Restart the kernel so NUMBA_NUM_THREADS=1 applies before import.")

## Unified experiment configuration

All three dataset notebooks use the same deterministic workflow: seed 42, 10 neighbours, two embedding dimensions, Euclidean distance, PaCMAP-family `MN_ratio=0.5`, `FP_ratio=2.0`, learning rate 1.0, and optimization phases of $(100, 100, 250)$. The following cell declares these shared settings.

--- Cell 4 [code] ---
# Unified deterministic benchmark parameters, matched to the prototype CMiO contract
SEED = 42
N_NEIGHBORS = 10
N_COMPONENTS = 2

K_VALUES = range(2, 11)

    "TA_PPM", "ZR_PPM", "CR_PPM", "PR_PPM", "ND_PPM", "ER_PPM", "EU_PPM",
    "SM_PPM", "YB_PPM", "TB_PPM", "HO_PPM", "GD_PPM", "DY_PPM", "LU_PPM",
    "BA_PPM", "SIO2_WT_PERCENT", "TIO2_WT_PERCENT", "NA2O_WT_PERCENT",
    "K2O_WT_PERCENT", "AL2O3_WT_PERCENT", "TM_PPM",
]

## Data inspection

The CMiO input contains 1,551 rows, 35 geochemical variables, and raw metre-based XYZ coordinates. During preparation, negative source codes representing below-detection-limit assays were replaced by half their encoded limit. The data contract below verifies the complete population, feature schema, labels, and finite nonnegative numeric values before any embedding is fitted.

--- Cell 6 [code] ---
DATA_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "processed" / "cmio_sc_pacmap_ready.csv"
data = pd.read_csv(DATA_PATH)

assert data.shape == (1551, 42)
assert data.columns.tolist() == METADATA_COLUMNS + FEATURE_COLUMNS

assert not data[METADATA_COLUMNS + FEATURE_COLUMNS].isna().any().any()

features_raw = data[FEATURE_COLUMNS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
coordinates_raw = data[XYZ_COLUMNS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
labels_true = data["label_id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
label_names = data[LABEL_COLUMN].astype(str).to_numpy()

assert not (features_raw < 0).any()

assert set(np.unique(labels_true)) == set(EXPECTED_LABELS)
observed_labels = (
    data[["label_id", LABEL_COLUMN]]
    .drop_duplicates()
    .set_index("label_id")[LABEL_COLUMN]
    .to_dict()
)
assert observed_labels == EXPECTED_LABELS

# Match the prototype benchmark's feature precision and TSS calculation.
feature_scaler = StandardScaler()
coordinate_scaler = StandardScaler()
features_standardized = feature_scaler.fit_transform(features_raw).astype(np.float32)
coordinates_standardized = coordinate_scaler.fit_transform(coordinates_raw)
features_with_xyz = np.column_stack([features_standardized, coordinates_standardized])
feature_center = features_standardized.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
total_sum_squares = float(np.sum((features_standardized - feature_center) ** 2))
assert total_sum_squares > 0

contract = pd.Series({
    "rows": len(data),
    "geochemical features": len(FEATURE_COLUMNS),
    "coordinate columns": len(XYZ_COLUMNS),
    "known classes": data[LABEL_COLUMN].nunique(),
    "duplicate sample IDs": int(data["sample_id"].duplicated().sum()),
    "missing required values": int(data[METADATA_COLUMNS + FEATURE_COLUMNS].isna().sum().sum()),
    "negative retained values": int((data[FEATURE_COLUMNS] < 0).sum().sum()),
}, name="CMiO data contract")
display(contract.to_frame())
display(
    data.groupby(["label_id", LABEL_COLUMN], observed=True)
    .size()
    .rename("samples")
    .reset_index()
)

--- Cell 9 [markdown] ---
## Candidate embeddings

All candidates use two output dimensions and seed 42. UMAP and the PaCMAP family use 10 neighbours and Euclidean distance; PaCMAP-family candidates additionally use learning rate 1.0, `MN_ratio=0.5`, `FP_ratio=2.0`, and optimization phases of $(100, 100, 250)$. SpaSNE uses its published defaults without label-guided tuning: $\alpha=10$, $\beta=5$, perplexity 50, exaggeration 4, $\theta=0.5$, PCA preprocessing, and 1000 iterations.

- **PaCMAP** uses standardized geochemistry only [Wang et al. (2021), *JMLR* 22(201)].
- **PaCMAP + XYZ** appends separately standardized XYZ coordinates to the geochemical matrix.
- **SC-PaCMAP** is the legacy benchmark: it replaces part of PaCMAP's fixed-size feature-neighbour graph with PCA-Geary-ranked geographic neighbours.
- **SC-PaCMAP (full graph)** keeps the complete original PaCMAP feature graph, adds a separate weighted PCA-Geary graph, and transfers a data-adaptive share of nearest-neighbour attraction to the SC loss during Phase 3.
- **SpaSNE** receives standardized geochemistry and raw XYZ separately, combining feature, global-feature, and spatial probability terms [Tang et al., *Dimensionality reduction for visualizing spatially resolved profiling data using SpaSNE*].
- **UMAP + XYZ** uses standardized geochemistry plus XYZ [McInnes, Healy, and Melville (2018), arXiv:1802.03426].
- **LocalMAP + XYZ** uses standardized geochemistry plus XYZ [Wang et al. (2025), *AAAI* 39(20), DOI: 10.1609/aaai.v39i20.35436].

The focused full-graph comparison uses the same six curves as the supplied reference figure: UMAP + XYZ, PaCMAP + XYZ, LocalMAP + XYZ, feature-only PaCMAP, replacement-graph SC-PaCMAP, and full-graph SC-PaCMAP. SpaSNE remains in the broader benchmark.

--- Cell 10 [markdown] ---
### PaCMAP implementation check

Before fitting the candidates, SC-PaCMAP with its spatial rule disabled must reproduce the copied PaCMAP implementation exactly. The copied implementation is also compared with the installed `pacmap==0.9.1` library.

--- Cell 12 [markdown] ---
### Fitted embeddings

Each candidate is fitted to the full population. The output table records input pathways and runtime; the following diagnostic view colors the embeddings by the evaluation-only deposit group for inspection.

--- Cell 13 [code] ---
METHOD_ORDER = [
    "UMAP + XYZ", "PaCMAP + XYZ", "LocalMAP + XYZ",
    "PaCMAP feature-only", "SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)",
    "SC-PaCMAP (full graph)", "SpaSNE",
]
FOCUSED_METHOD_ORDER = [
    "UMAP + XYZ", "PaCMAP + XYZ", "LocalMAP + XYZ",
    "PaCMAP feature-only", "SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)",
    "SC-PaCMAP (full graph)",
]
embeddings = {}
runtimes = {}


def store_embedding(method: str, embedding: np.ndarray, started_at: float) -> None:
    embedding = np.asarray(embedding, dtype=np.float64)
    assert embedding.shape == (len(data), N_COMPONENTS)
    assert np.isfinite(embedding).all()
    embeddings[method] = embedding
    runtimes[method] = time.perf_counter() - started_at


def fit_localmap_in_clean_process(x_input: np.ndarray, n_neighbors: int) -> np.ndarray:
    import subprocess

    worker_code = r'''
import os
os.environ["NUMBA_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
import importlib
import sys
import numba
import numpy as np
import pacmap

@numba.njit
def seed_numba_rng(random_state):
    np.random.seed(random_state)

pacmap_core = importlib.import_module("pacmap.pacmap")
original_resample = pacmap_core.sample_FP_pair_nearby
resample_call_index = 0

def deterministic_resample(X, pair_neighbors, old_pair_FP, Y, low_dist_thres):
    global resample_call_index
    seed_numba_rng(42 + resample_call_index)
    resample_call_index += 1
    return original_resample(X, pair_neighbors, old_pair_FP, Y, low_dist_thres)

pacmap_core.sample_FP_pair_nearby = deterministic_resample
rows, columns, n_neighbors = map(int, sys.argv[1:4])
x_input = np.frombuffer(sys.stdin.buffer.read(), dtype=np.float64).reshape(rows, columns)
seed_numba_rng(42)
model = pacmap.LocalMAP(
    n_components=2, n_neighbors=n_neighbors, MN_ratio=0.5, FP_ratio=2.0,
    distance="euclidean", lr=1.0, num_iters=(100, 100, 250),
    apply_pca=True, random_state=42, save_tree=False, low_dist_thres=10,
    knn_backend="faiss",
)
embedding = model.fit_transform(x_input, init="pca", save_pairs=False)
sys.stdout.buffer.write(np.asarray(embedding, dtype=np.float32).tobytes())
'''
    x_input = np.ascontiguousarray(x_input, dtype=np.float64)
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", worker_code, *map(str, x_input.shape), str(n_neighbors)],
        input=x_input.tobytes(), capture_output=True, check=False,
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(completed.stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    expected_bytes = len(x_input) * N_COMPONENTS * np.dtype(np.float32).itemsize
    if len(completed.stdout) != expected_bytes:
        raise RuntimeError("LocalMAP worker returned an unexpected embedding size.")
    return np.frombuffer(completed.stdout, dtype=np.float32).reshape(
        len(x_input), N_COMPONENTS
    ).copy()


started_at = time.perf_counter()
umap_model = umap.UMAP(
    n_neighbors=N_NEIGHBORS, n_components=N_COMPONENTS, metric="euclidean",
    learning_rate=1.0, random_state=SEED, n_jobs=1,
)
store_embedding("UMAP + XYZ", umap_model.fit_transform(features_with_xyz), started_at)

pacmap_parameters = {
    "n_components": N_COMPONENTS, "n_neighbors": N_NEIGHBORS,
    "MN_ratio": 0.5, "FP_ratio": 2.0, "distance": "euclidean",
    "lr": 1.0, "num_iters": (100, 100, 250), "apply_pca": True,
    "random_state": SEED, "save_tree": False, "knn_backend": "faiss",
}

started_at = time.perf_counter()
pacmap_xyz_model = pacmap.PaCMAP(**pacmap_parameters)
store_embedding(
    "PaCMAP + XYZ",
    pacmap_xyz_model.fit_transform(features_with_xyz, init="pca", save_pairs=False),
    started_at,
)

started_at = time.perf_counter()
store_embedding(
    "LocalMAP + XYZ",
    fit_localmap_in_clean_process(features_with_xyz, N_NEIGHBORS),
    started_at,
)

started_at = time.perf_counter()
pacmap_feature_model = pacmap.PaCMAP(**pacmap_parameters)
store_embedding(
    "PaCMAP feature-only",
    pacmap_feature_model.fit_transform(features_standardized, init="pca", save_pairs=False),
    started_at,
)

started_at = time.perf_counter()
replacement_sc_result, replacement_sc_diagnostics = run_legacy_sc_pacmap(
    features_standardized, coordinates_raw, settings
)
store_embedding("SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)", replacement_sc_result.embedding, started_at)
assert replacement_sc_diagnostics.k_spatial + replacement_sc_diagnostics.k_attribute == N_NEIGHBORS

started_at = time.perf_counter()
sc_result, sc_diagnostics = run_sc_pacmap(
    features_standardized, coordinates_raw, settings
)
store_embedding("SC-PaCMAP (full graph)", sc_result.embedding, started_at)
assert sc_diagnostics is not None
assert sc_diagnostics.k_attribute == N_NEIGHBORS
assert sc_result.pair_neighbors.shape == (len(data) * N_NEIGHBORS, 2)
assert sc_diagnostics.pair_sc.shape == (len(data) * sc_diagnostics.k_spatial, 2)
assert 0.0 <= sc_diagnostics.spatial_strength_lambda <= 1.0
assert 0.0 <= sc_diagnostics.automatic_target_share <= 0.95

started_at = time.perf_counter()
spasne_embedding = python_spasne(
    features_standardized,
    coordinates_raw,
    random_state=SEED,
    alpha=SPASNE_ALPHA,
    beta=SPASNE_BETA,
    exaggeration=SPASNE_EXAGGERATION,
    perplexity=SPASNE_PERPLEXITY,
    theta=SPASNE_THETA,
    max_iter=SPASNE_MAX_ITER,
    use_pca=True,
    spasne_dir=SPASNE_DIR,
)
store_embedding("SpaSNE", spasne_embedding, started_at)

embedding_cluster_inputs = embeddings
runtime_table = pd.DataFrame({
    "method": METHOD_ORDER,
    "seconds": [runtimes[method] for method in METHOD_ORDER],
    "geochemical input columns": [35] * len(METHOD_ORDER),
    "coordinate pathway": [
        "standardized XYZ columns", "standardized XYZ columns",
        "standardized XYZ columns", "none",
        "raw XYZ neighbour replacement graph", "raw XYZ separate Phase-3 SC graph",
        "raw XYZ spatial objective",
    ],
})
display(runtime_table.round({"seconds": 3}))

--- Cell 16 [markdown] ---
## Evaluation measures

K-means receives each raw 2-D embedding directly, without further standardization, normalization, rotation, or alignment. Spatial continuity uses each sample's 10 nearest non-self raw-XYZ neighbours:

$$\frac{H}{N} = -\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_j p_{ij}\log p_{ij}.$$

Geochemical delineation uses the standardized feature space:

$$\frac{\mathrm{WCSS}}{\mathrm{TSS}} = \frac{\sum_k\sum_{i \in k}\lVert x_i-\bar{x}_k\rVert^2}{\sum_i\lVert x_i-\bar{x}\rVert^2}.$$

Lower entropy and WCSS/TSS are preferable; ARI is an evaluation-only deposit-group reference computed on the same full population.

--- Cell 17 [markdown] ---
## Result board

For each embedding and each $K=2,\ldots,10$, deterministic K-means uses 20 initializations. The board reports spatial continuity, geochemical delineation, and evaluation-only deposit-group agreement for the complete candidate set.

--- Cell 18 [code] ---
# Prototype-matched evaluation: raw 2-D embeddings enter K-means unchanged.
spatial_model = NearestNeighbors(metric="euclidean", n_jobs=1).fit(coordinates_raw)
spatial_neighbors = spatial_model.kneighbors(
    coordinates_raw, n_neighbors=N_NEIGHBORS + 1, return_distance=False,
)[:, 1:]
assert spatial_neighbors.shape == (len(data), N_NEIGHBORS)

spatial_context = pd.Series({
    "raw-XYZ neighbors per sample": N_NEIGHBORS,
    "focal sample excluded": True,
    "K-means embedding input": "raw 2-D embedding (not standardized)",
}, name="prototype-matched evaluation context")
display(spatial_context.to_frame())


def total_windowed_entropy(cluster_labels: np.ndarray) -> float:
    total = 0.0
    for neighbors in spatial_neighbors:
        counts = np.bincount(cluster_labels[neighbors])
        probabilities = counts[counts > 0] / N_NEIGHBORS
        total -= float(np.sum(probabilities * np.log(probabilities)))
    return total


def feature_space_wcss(cluster_labels: np.ndarray, n_clusters: int) -> float:
    centroids = np.vstack([
        features_standardized[cluster_labels == cluster_id].mean(axis=0)
        for cluster_id in range(n_clusters)
    ])
    residuals = features_standardized - centroids[cluster_labels]
    return float(np.sum(residuals * residuals))


result_rows = []
for method in METHOD_ORDER:
    raw_embedding = embeddings[method]
    assert raw_embedding.shape == (len(data), N_COMPONENTS)
    for k in K_VALUES:
        cluster_labels = KMeans(
            n_clusters=k,
            init="k-means++",
            n_init=20,
            random_state=1000 + k,
        ).fit_predict(raw_embedding)
        result_rows.append({
            "method": method,
            "K": k,
            "H_per_sample": total_windowed_entropy(cluster_labels) / len(data),
            "WCSS_TSS": feature_space_wcss(cluster_labels, k) / total_sum_squares,
            "ARI": adjusted_rand_score(labels_true, cluster_labels),
        })

results = pd.DataFrame(result_rows)
results["method"] = pd.Categorical(
    results["method"], categories=METHOD_ORDER, ordered=True
)
results = results.sort_values(["method", "K"]).reset_index(drop=True)

assert results.columns.tolist() == ["method", "K", "H_per_sample", "WCSS_TSS", "ARI"]
assert len(results) == len(METHOD_ORDER) * len(list(K_VALUES))
assert not results.duplicated(["method", "K"]).any()
assert results.groupby("method", observed=True)["K"].apply(
    lambda values: list(values) == list(K_VALUES)
).all()
assert np.isfinite(results[["H_per_sample", "WCSS_TSS", "ARI"]]).all().all()
assert results["H_per_sample"].ge(0).all() and results["WCSS_TSS"].gt(0).all()
assert results["ARI"].between(-1, 1).all()

display(results.round({"H_per_sample": 4, "WCSS_TSS": 4, "ARI": 4}))

].copy()

fingerprint_table["method"] = fingerprint_table["method"].astype(str)
fingerprint_payload = fingerprint_table.to_csv(
    index=False, float_format="%.12g", lineterminator="\n"
).encode("utf-8")

--- Cell 25 [markdown] ---
## Full seven-method benchmark

This section retains SpaSNE in addition to the six-method full-graph comparison. The input pathways remain explicit: the three `+ XYZ` candidates use standardized coordinates as ordinary input columns; feature-only PaCMAP receives no coordinates; replacement-graph SC-PaCMAP uses raw XYZ to replace part of its fixed-size NN graph; full-graph SC-PaCMAP retains the full feature graph and uses raw XYZ only for a separate Phase-3 SC loss; SpaSNE receives raw XYZ separately through its spatial objective.

        axis.axhline(0, color="black", linewidth=1, linestyle=":")
    axis.set(
        xlabel="K-means clusters (K)",
        ylabel=ylabel,
        title=title,
    )
    axis.set_xticks(list(K_VALUES))
    axis.grid(alpha=0.25)
handles, legend_labels = axes[0].get_legend_handles_labels()
fig.legend(handles, legend_labels, loc="outside lower center", ncol=4, frameon=False)
fig.suptitle("Full seven-method benchmark", fontsize=15)
plt.show()

--- Cell 27 [markdown] ---
### Full benchmark trade-off

Each point is a method-by-$K$ solution; marker size increases with $K$, and the black ring identifies the seven-class reference. The lower-left direction is preferable jointly, but no single scalar is used to collapse the two evaluation objectives.

--- Cell 28 [code] ---
# All-candidate spatial-geochemical trade-off
_, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(10.5, 6.8))
for method in METHOD_ORDER:
    method_results = results.loc[results["method"] == method].sort_values("K")
    axis.plot(

--- Cell 29 [markdown] ---
## Summary at the seven-class reference

The summary below identifies the best observed value for each metric at the label-informed $K=7$ reference and quantifies the SC-PaCMAP change relative to feature-only PaCMAP. ARI is an in-sample, evaluation-only agreement measure, not held-out or independent validation.

--- Cell 30 [code] ---
best_entropy = known_k_results.loc[known_k_results["H_per_sample"].idxmin()]
best_wcss = known_k_results.loc[known_k_results["WCSS_TSS"].idxmin()]
best_ari = known_k_results.loc[known_k_results["ARI"].idxmax()]
overall_ari_best = results.loc[results["ARI"].idxmax()]
full_graph_known = known_k_results.loc[
    known_k_results["method"] == "SC-PaCMAP (full graph)"
] .iloc[0]
replacement_graph_known = known_k_results.loc[
    known_k_results["method"] == "SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)"
] .iloc[0]
feature_known = known_k_results.loc[
    known_k_results["method"] == "PaCMAP feature-only"
] .iloc[0]
entropy_reduction = 100 * (
    feature_known["H_per_sample"] - full_graph_known["H_per_sample"]
) / feature_known["H_per_sample"]
wcss_change = 100 * (
    full_graph_known["WCSS_TSS"] - feature_known["WCSS_TSS"]
) / feature_known["WCSS_TSS"]
replacement_graph_wcss_recovery = 100 * (
    replacement_graph_known["WCSS_TSS"] - full_graph_known["WCSS_TSS"]
) / replacement_graph_known["WCSS_TSS"]

print(
    f"At K={KNOWN_CLASS_COUNT}, {best_entropy['method']} has the lowest entropy "
    f"H/N ({best_entropy['H_per_sample']:.4f}), {best_wcss['method']} has the lowest "
    f"WCSS/TSS ({best_wcss['WCSS_TSS']:.4f}), and {best_ari['method']} has the highest "
    f"ARI ({best_ari['ARI']:.4f})."
)
print(
    f"Relative to feature-only PaCMAP at K={KNOWN_CLASS_COUNT}, automatic-share "
    f"full-graph SC-PaCMAP reduces entropy by {entropy_reduction:.1f}% while changing "
    f"WCSS by {wcss_change:+.1f}%."
)
print(
    f"Relative to replacement-graph SC-PaCMAP, the full-graph method lowers WCSS/TSS by "
    f"{replacement_graph_wcss_recovery:.1f}%."
)
print(
    f"The largest ARI in the complete sweep is {overall_ari_best['ARI']:.4f} from "
    f"{overall_ari_best['method']} at K={int(overall_ari_best['K'])}."
)

--- Cell 32 [markdown] ---
## All-K stability bands

Every method is refit with algorithm seeds 11, 42, and 97 and evaluated across $K=2,\ldots,10$. The solid line is the across-seed median and the shaded band is the min-max range at each $K$. These bands describe optimizer sensitivity on this one fixed CMiO dataset; they are **not confidence intervals**, and no population-level significance test is applied.

--- Cell 33 [code] ---
ALGORITHM_SEEDS = (11, 42, 97)
STABILITY_METHOD_ORDER = list(METHOD_ORDER)


def fit_localmap_for_seed(x_input: np.ndarray, algorithm_seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    import subprocess

    worker_code = r'''
import importlib
import os
import sys
os.environ["NUMBA_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
import numba
import numpy as np
import pacmap

@numba.njit
def seed_numba_rng(random_state):
    np.random.seed(random_state)

rows, columns, n_neighbors, algorithm_seed = map(int, sys.argv[1:5])
pacmap_core = importlib.import_module("pacmap.pacmap")
original_resample = pacmap_core.sample_FP_pair_nearby
resample_call_index = 0

def deterministic_resample(X, pair_neighbors, old_pair_FP, Y, low_dist_thres):
    global resample_call_index
    seed_numba_rng(algorithm_seed + resample_call_index)
    resample_call_index += 1
    return original_resample(X, pair_neighbors, old_pair_FP, Y, low_dist_thres)

pacmap_core.sample_FP_pair_nearby = deterministic_resample
x_input = np.frombuffer(sys.stdin.buffer.read(), dtype=np.float64).reshape(rows, columns)
seed_numba_rng(algorithm_seed)
model = pacmap.LocalMAP(
    n_components=2, n_neighbors=n_neighbors, MN_ratio=0.5, FP_ratio=2.0,
    distance="euclidean", lr=1.0, num_iters=(100, 100, 250),
    apply_pca=True, random_state=algorithm_seed, save_tree=False,
    low_dist_thres=10, knn_backend="faiss",
)
embedding = model.fit_transform(x_input, init="pca", save_pairs=False)
sys.stdout.buffer.write(np.asarray(embedding, dtype=np.float32).tobytes())
'''
    x_input = np.ascontiguousarray(x_input, dtype=np.float64)
    arguments = [*map(str, x_input.shape), str(N_NEIGHBORS), str(algorithm_seed)]
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", worker_code, *arguments],
        input=x_input.tobytes(), capture_output=True, check=False,
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(completed.stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    return np.frombuffer(completed.stdout, dtype=np.float32).reshape(
        len(x_input), N_COMPONENTS
    ).copy()


def fit_embeddings_for_seed(algorithm_seed: int) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    if algorithm_seed == SEED:
        return embeddings

    seed_settings = PaCMAPSettings(
        n_components=N_COMPONENTS, n_neighbors=N_NEIGHBORS,
        mn_ratio=0.5, fp_ratio=2.0, learning_rate=1.0,
        num_iters=(100, 100, 250), random_state=algorithm_seed,
        knn_backend="faiss", verbose=False,
    )
    seed_pacmap_parameters = {**pacmap_parameters, "random_state": algorithm_seed}
    seed_embeddings = {
        "UMAP + XYZ": umap.UMAP(
            n_neighbors=N_NEIGHBORS, n_components=N_COMPONENTS,
            metric="euclidean", learning_rate=1.0,
            random_state=algorithm_seed, n_jobs=1,
        ).fit_transform(features_with_xyz),
        "PaCMAP + XYZ": pacmap.PaCMAP(**seed_pacmap_parameters).fit_transform(
            features_with_xyz, init="pca", save_pairs=False
        ),
        "LocalMAP + XYZ": fit_localmap_for_seed(features_with_xyz, algorithm_seed),
        "PaCMAP feature-only": pacmap.PaCMAP(**seed_pacmap_parameters).fit_transform(
            features_standardized, init="pca", save_pairs=False
        ),
    }
    replacement_result, _ = run_legacy_sc_pacmap(
        features_standardized, coordinates_raw, seed_settings
    )
    full_graph_result, _ = run_sc_pacmap(
        features_standardized, coordinates_raw, seed_settings
    )
    seed_embeddings["SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)"] = replacement_result.embedding
    seed_embeddings["SC-PaCMAP (full graph)"] = full_graph_result.embedding
    seed_embeddings["SpaSNE"] = python_spasne(
        features_standardized, coordinates_raw, random_state=algorithm_seed,
        alpha=SPASNE_ALPHA, beta=SPASNE_BETA,
        exaggeration=SPASNE_EXAGGERATION, perplexity=SPASNE_PERPLEXITY,
        theta=SPASNE_THETA, max_iter=SPASNE_MAX_ITER, use_pca=True,
        spasne_dir=SPASNE_DIR,
    )
    return seed_embeddings


def evaluate_partition(
    method: str, algorithm_seed: int, k: int, cluster_labels: np.ndarray
) -> dict:
    return {
        "method": method,
        "algorithm_seed": algorithm_seed,
        "K": k,
        "H_per_sample": total_windowed_entropy(cluster_labels) / len(data),
        "WCSS_TSS": feature_space_wcss(cluster_labels, k) / total_sum_squares,
        "ARI": adjusted_rand_score(labels_true, cluster_labels),
    }


stability_rows = []
for algorithm_seed in ALGORITHM_SEEDS:
    seed_embeddings = fit_embeddings_for_seed(algorithm_seed)
    for method in METHOD_ORDER:
        for k in K_VALUES:
            labels = KMeans(
                n_clusters=k, init="k-means++", n_init=20, random_state=1000 + k,
            ).fit_predict(seed_embeddings[method])
            stability_rows.append(evaluate_partition(method, algorithm_seed, k, labels))

stability_results = pd.DataFrame(stability_rows)
expected_rows = len(K_VALUES) * len(METHOD_ORDER) * len(ALGORITHM_SEEDS)
assert len(stability_results) == expected_rows == 189
assert not stability_results.duplicated(["method", "algorithm_seed", "K"]).any()
assert np.isfinite(stability_results[["H_per_sample", "WCSS_TSS", "ARI"]]).all().all()

output_path = ROOT / "results" / "cmio_algorithm_stability.csv"
output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
stability_results.to_csv(output_path, index=False)
display(stability_results.round(4))
print(f"Saved {len(stability_results)} rows to {output_path.relative_to(ROOT)}")

    "PaCMAP + XYZ": ("#56B4E9", "^", "-"),

            linestyle=linestyle, linewidth=2.8 if is_primary else 1.5,
            markersize=8 if is_primary else 5, label=method,
        )

from pathlib import Path
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd


def find_results_root() -> Path:
    candidates = [Path.cwd(), *Path.cwd().parents]
    for candidate in candidates:
        if (candidate / "results" / "cmio_algorithm_stability.csv").is_file():
            return candidate
        nested = candidate / "sc-pacmap-reproducibility"
        if (nested / "results" / "cmio_algorithm_stability.csv").is_file():
            return nested
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not locate cmio_algorithm_stability.csv")


stability_results = pd.read_csv(
    find_results_root() / "results" / "cmio_algorithm_stability.csv"
)
method_styles = {
    "UMAP + XYZ": ("#D55E00", "o", "-"),
    "PaCMAP + XYZ": ("#56B4E9", "^", "-"),
    "LocalMAP + XYZ": ("#009E73", "v", "-"),
    "PaCMAP feature-only": ("#7A5195", "P", "-"),
    "SC-PaCMAP (neighbour replacement graph)": ("#E15759", "h", "-"),
    "SC-PaCMAP (full graph)": ("#E60049", "*", "-"),
    "SpaSNE": ("#D4A900", "X", "-"),
}
method_order = list(method_styles)
reference_k = 7

fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.5, 7.5), constrained_layout=True)
for method in method_order:
    rows = stability_results.loc[stability_results["method"] == method]
    color, marker, linestyle = method_styles[method]
    is_primary = method == "SC-PaCMAP (full graph)"

    # One faint colored line per seed, instead of a solid shaded box per K.
    for seed in ALGORITHM_SEEDS:
        seed_rows = rows.loc[rows["algorithm_seed"] == seed].sort_values("K")
        axis.plot(
            seed_rows["H_per_sample"], seed_rows["WCSS_TSS"], color=color,
            linewidth=1.0, alpha=0.28, zorder=1,
        )

    summary = rows.groupby("K")[["H_per_sample", "WCSS_TSS"]].median()
    sizes = 28 + 7 * (summary.index - summary.index.min())
    axis.plot(
        summary["H_per_sample"], summary["WCSS_TSS"], color=color,
        linestyle=linestyle, linewidth=3.0 if is_primary else 1.7,
        zorder=2, label=method,
    )
    axis.scatter(
        summary["H_per_sample"], summary["WCSS_TSS"], s=sizes,
        color=color, marker=marker,
        edgecolor="black" if is_primary else color,
        linewidth=0.9 if is_primary else 0, zorder=3,
    )

    if is_primary:
        for k in sorted({int(summary.index.min()), reference_k, int(summary.index.max())}):
            axis.annotate(
                f"K={k}", (summary.loc[k, "H_per_sample"], summary.loc[k, "WCSS_TSS"]),
                xytext=(6, 6), textcoords="offset points", color=color,
                fontsize=9, fontweight="bold",
            )

axis.set(
    xlabel="Mean windowed entropy H/N (lower is better)",
    ylabel="Feature-space WCSS/TSS (lower is better)",
    title="CMiO all-K spatial-feature trade-off range",
)
axis.grid(alpha=0.2)
axis.legend(
    title="Faint lines: seed runs; bold line: seed median",
    bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), loc="upper left",
)
plt.show()
